# Desktop Parity Implementation Plan: Context Breakdown, the `+` Menu, the /usage Card, the Second Mac Test, the `+` Menu by Source and Session Tabs

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** In the side chat, as the desktop app does but sized for a tool window: the context ring opens a breakdown of the context window by category with its MCP tools, memory files and skills; `/usage` (and `/cost`) draws a card with this session's cost, times and per-model tokens and what has used the plan's limits on this machine; and `+` opens a paged menu with Add files or photos, Add folder (the CLI's `--add-dir` on the next spawn), Slash commands, Connectors (the MCP panel) and the session's plugins with their commands. Every number comes from the CLI's own output.

**Goal (spec 12 to 16, the owner's second Mac test):** while Claude works, Enter sends a message the CLI reads at its next step and Ctrl+Enter (⌘↵ on macOS) queues one for after the turn, each a pending bubble until the CLI starts it, with Edit and Cancel on a queued one, Stop withdrawing what waits, and folded messages kept on reload; every ended turn's reply can be forked, cut there with `--resume-session-at`; Shift+Tab in the composer cycles the permission mode through a Keymap action and Tab moves the focus forward; a light band sweeps the working row's label beside 0.1.2's orbs, which stay as shipped, and a streaming thinking row's label, both still under `Reduce motion`; the /usage card shows each model's share of the cost, web searches, the request count and every tip, and the ring leads to it. The owner's words: "I can't queue messages, it doesn't let me [send] anything till the steps are over ... instead of allowing me to send it and then it queues it like in the cli/desktop app", "The fork should be allowed from everywhere also, otherwise we are over promising, the Claude code desktop app allows for forking all throughout", "I want a keybind to change modes while in a chat", "There is no cool animation like on Claude code for the thinking", "The usage doesn't do anything and I want to show more in detail".

**Goal (spec 17 and 18, the owner's test of 0.2.0):** the `+` menu splits what it offers by where it comes from, as the desktop's does: every page-1 row with its icon, `Add files or photos` with its shortcut (a Keymap action, Ctrl+Alt+Shift+O, ⌘⌥⇧O on macOS), `Connectors ›` with a switch per claude.ai connector on the MCP panel's own controller, `Browse connectors` and `Manage connectors`, and `Plugins ›` with each plugin under the name the CLI gives it and its `Skills` apart from its `Commands`, every list there before the first message; and the tool window's header gets one tab per live session, the selected one and any that needs the user, with the sidebar's status dot, a `+`, the platform's close buttons, keys and overflow, kept in step with the sidebar both ways. The owner's words: "The plus besides the file just shows slash commands I want it to be more pretty and actually divide it per where it gets it from like in the desktop app", "Also add multiple tabs at the top for active sessions instead of just chat, there is free real estate there."

**Architecture:** D1 types the three answers (`get_context_usage`, `get_usage`, `init.plugins` / `additional_directories`) in `protocol/` with a fixture test per shape, and `SessionConfig` learns `--add-dir`. D2a gives `ClaudeSession` the two requests and the `/usage` fetch, the reducer the usage card's part states and the breakdown, `SessionManager` the coalesced `contextBreakdown` and `retryUsage`, and the fake CLI its inline answers; D2b gives `SessionHandle` its added folders, the respawn when idle and the init confirmation. D3 adds the pure models in `chat/` (`contextPopover`, `usageCard`, `pluginMenu`); D4 draws the popover on a `ContextBreakdownController`, D5 the card, D6a the paged `+` menu, D6b Add folder and its pills; D0 records `--add-dir` with a real CLI first, and D7 writes the docs and runs every suite.

**Architecture (spec 12 to 16):** D8 scripts the committed queue and fork stories and records six more (a steer after the last tool round, a withdrawn steer, a fork while the original's process is live and one while it runs a turn, a slash command and an image sent `later` while a turn runs, a `later` message while a permission card waits), and scripts the parity requests for the contract job; D9 types `command_lifecycle`, the queue frames and their replies, and `SessionConfig.resumeSessionAt`; D10 tracks messages sent while busy in the session layer (pending and held sends, withdraw, Stop with `cancel_queued`, returned messages, folded `queued_command` lines on reload, attention and idle) and teaches the fake rule (m); D12a gives the reducer a fork point per reply and `SessionManager.fork` a cut, D13a the mode cycle's locked pick and refused modes; D11 draws the queue (`QueuedRow`, the keys, Send beside Stop, Edit and Cancel), D12b the hover row's fork on every reply, D13b the `Cycle Permission Mode` action and Tab's forward move, D14 the shimmer beside 0.1.2's orbs and a drawn-motion test of both. Section 16 rides on D1 (`webSearches`), D3 (`costShare`, every tip, the request count), D4 (`Show session usage`) and D5 (the bars, the tips' disclosure), marked (spec 16) there; D0 also records a fork at a reply with `--add-dir` and D2b gives such a fork its parent's folders, marked (spec 13).

**Architecture (spec 17 and 18):** the parts that overlap the `+` menu of sections 3.3 and 3.5 are folded into the tasks that build that menu, marked (spec 17): D1 decodes `init.skills`, D2a keeps it in `ChatState.skills`, D3 writes 17.5's `pluginMenu` (display-name labels, `Skills` apart from `Commands`, `anthropic-skills` never a plugin) and `connectorMenu`, and D6a draws page 1 with its five icons, → unbound, and the Plugins pages by source. Two new Opus tasks follow D14: D15 the Connectors page on `McpPanelController`, the drawn switch, `Browse connectors` and `Manage connectors`, and the `Tandem.AddFilesOrPhotos` action with the row's shortcut text; D16 the session tabs on the tool window's own `ContentManager` with one shared Compose panel, `chat/SessionTabs.kt`, `SessionTabsController`, the tab icon, `Tandem.NewSession`, the neighbour rule of a close, and the attention of a process that ends by itself.

**Tech Stack:** Kotlin 2.4.20, IntelliJ Platform 2026.1.5 (Jewel 0.37.0, build 261.27258.48), kotlinx.serialization.json tree API, the platform's kotlinx.coroutines and kotlinx.collections.immutable (never bundled, hard rule 7), JUnit 5 (+ vintage for the JUnit 4 classes), IDE Starter + UI Driver, the Python fake CLI and recording scripts.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-desktop-parity-design.md` (sections 3, 4, 9 and 10, 12 to 16 for the second Mac test, and 17 and 18 with 9.8 for the test of 0.2.0, are the contract; 7 the edge cases; 11 the open items). Probes 6 (the catalogue's sources, `init.plugins`, `init.skills`; its `ps:N` lines are a scratch run, never cited by a test) for section 17. Companions: `docs/superpowers/research/2026-10-22-desktop-parity-probes.md` (probes), `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-mcp-controls-design.md` (MCP: 3.2, 3.4, 3.5, 4.2), `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-chat-polish-design.md` (polish: 2.3, 2.7), `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-21-mcp-controls.md` (the request path and fake rules (a)–(g) this plan extends), `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-chat-redesign-design.md` (chat-redesign: 4.9 the working row and 4.9.1 its orbs, 4.18 the keyboard map); the probes' section 5 (the `fork-at` and `queue-*` fixtures).

**Base:** implementation of this round starts later, from `feat/mcp-controls` once it has landed and has merged `feat/chat-redesign` at `e6e8fee`, Tandem 0.1.2 (its 0.1.1 fixes, `6cb1973^..8317665`, that is `6cb1973` to `8317665` inclusive, with `main` merged in: `ComposerImages.chooseImagesLater` and `chooseLater` with `ImageChooserTest` (`6cb1973`, `6372695`), `FooterControls.focusLeft`, the `Reduce motion` setting, `LocalReducedMotion`, `workingView` and the composed `WorkingRowItemTest` (`754d1eb`); then 0.1.2's, `31b5d3e..e6e8fee`: the working row's orbs, `chat/OrbIndicator` drawn by `ui/rows/Spark(animated, tint)` with `OrbIndicatorTest` (chat-redesign 4.9.1), which the owner approved after testing 0.1.2; the models a session offers before its own process lists any, `SessionManager.models` (the last listed, seeded from the workspace), `composerModel(state, known)`, `modelDisplayName(id, models)` and `ChatPresenters(scope, options, models, send)`; and `main` merged in again, which runs the IDE test in three CI shards (`IdeTestShard`, `tandem.ideTestShard`: a new test in `ToolWindowIdeTest` joins a shard by itself, a new IDE test class needs `@ExtendWith(IdeTestShard::class)`, which `scripts/test_workflows.py` pins, and a local `integrationTest` without the property runs every test)), not from `522c3e2`; the narrower range `dc3061f..8317665` would leave the chooser fix out. Sections 12 and 14 (D10, D11, D13b) also build on `feat/mcp-controls`' bash-mode UI, `604574b` (`feat: bash mode in the chat`) or later, which `522c3e2` lacks: `ComposerModel.shellRunning`, `stopTarget`, `PromptBar`'s `busy = composer.busy || composer.shellRunning` and its `shellCommand` → `manager.runShell` send path (the session side, `heldEscapes`, `pendingOutput`, `ChatState.quietSends`, `sendShellEscape` and `runShellJob`, is in `522c3e2` already). Before D1 the orchestrator moves `feat/desktop-parity` onto that commit (it rebases this branch's own commits: docs, fixtures, the redactor's queue keys and the decode test's transcript list; no product code), and every task assumes those pieces are there: a task that finds `ComposerImages.chooseImagesLater`, `FooterControls.focusLeft`, `TandemSettings.State.reduceMotion`, `LocalReducedMotion`, `workingView`, `WorkingRowItemTest`, `OrbIndicator`, `SessionManager.models`, `composerModel(state, known)`, `modelDisplayName`, `ComposerModel.shellRunning`, `stopTarget` or `PromptBar`'s `shellCommand` send path missing stops and reports. D14 builds on `LocalReducedMotion`, `workingView`, `WorkingRowItemTest` and the orbs, which it leaves as they are; D3 and D11 extend `composerModel(state, known)`, D11 keeps `ChatPresenters`' `models` (`SessionManager.models`) and D3 names the card's models with `modelDisplayName`, all with 0.1.2's signatures; D6b opens its chooser the way `chooseImagesLater` does. The spec's sections 12 to 16 (slices D8 to D14) are tasks D8 to D14 below, D12 and D13 each split into a session task (a) and a chat and ui task (b) (deviation 14). Sections 17 and 18 (D3, D6a, D15, D16) also build on PR #9 (`feat/mcp-controls`), which lands on `main` before this round starts, and in it on its fix of the lists that were empty before a session's first message (`b6688c8`, `fix(session,ui): a resumed session's palette and MCP panel before its first prompt`, on the local `feat/mcp-controls` when this plan was written): `SessionManager.commands` (a `CommandCatalogue(commands, terminal)`, seeded from `TandemWorkspaceState.knownCommands`), `composerModel(state, known, knownCommands)` (a session's own catalogue, else the manager's), `ChatPresenters(scope, options, models, send, commands)`, `McpControls.knownMcpServers`, `McpControls.startForMcp` and `McpPanelController`'s start of a stopped session (`McpPanelStart`, `mcpPanelNotice`). So the implementation base is `main` with PR #9 merged; every task assumes those pieces too, and a task that finds `SessionManager.commands`, `CommandCatalogue`, `composerModel`'s `knownCommands`, `knownMcpServers`, `startForMcp` or `McpPanelStart` missing stops and reports. Where a task below says `composerModel(state, known)` it means that function with PR #9's `knownCommands` parameter kept as it is.

**Dependencies and tiers:** two recording tasks, D8 → D0 (both edit `scripts/drive_cli.py`, and D0's fork at a reply uses D8's `fork_at_argv` and `reply_uuid`), run beside the Kotlin chain D1 → D9 → D2a → D10 → D12a → D13a → D3 → D4 → D5 → D6a → D11 → D12b → D13b → D14 → D15 → D16 → D2b → D6b → D7 → whole-branch review: protocol and session first, then chat, then ui (the tasks with IDE Starter tests: D4, D5, D6a, D11, D12b, D13b, D14 (a screenshot; IDE test 5 only under deviation 19), D15, D16, D6b), then docs. D15 and D16 wait on no recording, so they run before D2b and D6b, which wait on D0 (deviation 22). D0 and D8 need a real signed-in `claude` and may take a while, so the chain waits only where it must: D11 starts once D8 has committed its `queue-steer-text`, `queue-cancel-steer`, `queue-later-command` and `queue-during-card` outcomes, D12b once D8 has committed its `fork-at-live` and `fork-at-busy` outcomes, D2b and D6b once D0 has committed its outcome (they run last for that reason). D14 needs nothing from this round, so it moves to any point where the chain waits for a recording, as a chain step: between two chain commits, on a clean tree, never beside another Kotlin task (it shares `ide/TandemSessions.kt` with D6b, `ui/rows/` with D5 and D11 and `ToolWindowIdeTest.kt` with the IDE-test tasks). The recording tasks share the chain's worktree, so a recording task touches the tracked tree only in a gap between two chain tasks: it records into `scratch/` (gitignored) whenever it likes, but moves fixtures into `src/test/resources`, edits a tracked file, runs Gradle and commits only after a chain task's commit and before the next chain task starts, with `git status --short` empty; the chain resumes after the recording task's commit. So no chain task runs `check` while a recording task's uncommitted fixtures sit in `src/test/resources` (the generic sweeps read every fixture there), a recording task's Gradle run sees no half-edited chain code, and its commit holds only its own files. Shared files fix the rest: D9 after D1 (`HostMessages`, `SessionConfig`); D10 after D2a (`Reducer`, `ChatState`, `ClaudeSession`); D12a and D13a after D10 (`Reducer`, `SessionHandle`, `SessionManager`); D11 after D6a (`PromptBar`, `ComposerKeys`); D12b after D11 (`ChatRowsBuilder`, `ChatPresenter`); D13b after D12b (`ComposerKeys`, `PromptBar`, `FooterControls`); D2b after D12a (its `fork()` copies folders to a fork at a reply too); D15 after D13b (`plugin.xml`'s `<actions>`, `ChatUiState.openMenus`, `PromptBar`) and D6a (`PagedMenu.kt`, the `+` menu's pages); D16 after D15 (`plugin.xml`, `ToolWindowIdeTest.kt`) and after D10 and D12b (`SessionManager`, `SessionHandle`, `SessionActions`); D2b after D16 (`SessionHandle`, `SessionManager`: D16's count of self-ended processes already leaves a respawn's `stopIfIdle` out); D6b after D15 (`PromptBar`, and D15's IDE test 1, whose page-1 rows gain `Add folder`). D4 and D5 both edit `NoticeItem.kt` and `ChatPanel.kt`, and D6a, D11, D13b, D15 and D6b all edit `PromptBar.kt`, so they run in order. Tiers (user CLAUDE.md, spec 10): D1, D2a, D2b, D3, D4, D5, D6a, D6b, D9, D10, D11, D12a, D12b, D13a, D13b, D14, D15, D16 and their per-task reviewers on Opus; D0 and D8 (recording tooling and fixtures) and D7's docs on Sonnet, as are small review fixes; a task that fails review twice moves to Fable; the whole-branch review after D7 runs on Fable.

Time matters here: do not spend time that can be avoided, and the earlier a correct result is obtained, the better.

## Global Constraints

- Time matters here: do not spend time that can be avoided, and the earlier a correct result is obtained, the better.
- CLAUDE.md hard rules, verbatim:
  1. **Never block the EDT.** No process I/O, file I/O, JSON parsing or waiting on the CLI from the UI thread. Use the platform coroutine scopes (`platform-coroutines-structured-concurrency` skill).
  2. **No Node, no Agent SDK at runtime.** We spawn the user's `claude` binary directly. Do not add a sidecar.
  3. **Never touch credentials.** Auth belongs to the CLI. Do not read `~/.claude/.credentials*`, keychains or API keys, and never offer a login flow.
  4. **Edits go through the IDE, not the disk.** Claude's built-in Edit/Write tools are disabled; our MCP edit tools apply changes with `WriteCommandAction` on the `Document`, so undo, unsaved buffers and local history work. Every edit shows a native diff before it lands, unless the user picked auto-accept.
  5. **Never fail silently.** If the CLI falls back to another model, rejects a model, retries, or errors, the user sees it in the chat. Compare the model in the `system/init` event (and fallback events) with what the user picked.
  6. **Every protocol change gets a fixture test.** Recorded transcripts live in `src/test/resources/fixtures/`. Unknown message types must parse to `Unknown(raw)`, never throw. `synthetic-long-reply.ndjson` is generated by `scripts/make_long_reply.py` and is the only unrecorded fixture.
  7. **Do not bundle kotlinx.coroutines or the Kotlin stdlib.** The platform provides both.
  8. **Do not call the product "Claude Code"**, in the plugin name, ID or UI. Use "Tandem" and say it works with Claude Code.
- Hard rule 3 for this round (spec 1): every number comes from the CLI's own output (a control response, a message, a local command's text, or a transcript the CLI wrote); Tandem never calls Anthropic's API and never computes an account limit; a part the CLI cannot give over stream-json is left out or deferred (spec 2.2), never computed.
- CLAUDE.md conventions, verbatim: "Validate everything that comes from the CLI; it is an external process that changes weekly." and "Never log prompt contents, file contents or secrets. Log message types and ids." Spec 4.2's rule: subtypes, request ids, counts and uuids only; never memory-file paths, tool, skill, server or plugin names, recommendation texts, folder paths or costs.
- Layers (CLAUDE.md Layout): `protocol/` imports nothing from IntelliJ; `session/` only the JDK, kotlinx.coroutines, kotlinx.serialization.json, kotlinx.collections.immutable and `com.intellij.openapi.diagnostic`; `chat/` only the JDK, kotlinx.collections.immutable, kotlinx.serialization.json and `session/`/`protocol/` types (no Compose, Jewel or IntelliJ). `koverVerify` holds each of the three at ≥ 90 % lines.
- Where the work happens: the git worktree `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-tandem/ee35bc45-05bb-55f2-a063-cf2c94eb6664/scratchpad/main-worktree` (called WT below) on `feat/desktop-parity`, cut from `origin/feat/mcp-controls` at `522c3e2` and moved onto the landed `feat/mcp-controls` (with 0.1.1's fixes and 0.1.2) before D1 (**Base** above). Every command runs from WT. `/home/user/tandem` is another branch in progress: never write there, never create another worktree, and read nothing from it during the round: before D0 and D1 the orchestrator copies, once, `/home/user/tandem/scratch/central-mirror.init.gradle.kts` (Gradle) and `/home/user/tandem/scratch/personal-names.txt` (the D7 check) into `WT/scratch/` (gitignored), and every command below uses those copies, so a change or cleanup in that tree mid-round breaks no task.
- Gradle: every command carries `--init-script scratch/central-mirror.init.gradle.kts` (the copy in `WT/scratch/`, relative to WT, where every command runs). One class: `./gradlew test --init-script scratch/central-mirror.init.gradle.kts --tests 'dev.daarkolabs.tandem.<pkg>.<Class>'`; `./gradlew check --init-script scratch/central-mirror.init.gradle.kts` adds `koverVerify`; IDE tests only in D4, D5, D6a, D6b, D11, D12b, D13b, D14, D15, D16 and D7: `xvfb-run ./gradlew integrationTest --init-script scratch/central-mirror.init.gradle.kts` (1.5–3 min each). Every Kotlin task ends with `check` green and one commit, on a tree that holds only its own changes (the recording tasks' gap rule, **Dependencies and tiers**). If a host is refused, stop and name it.
- Jewel 0.37.0 as bundled in 2026.1.5: `PopupMenu(onDismissRequest, popupPositionProvider, modifier, …) { MenuScope }` with `selectableItem`, `passiveItem`, `separator`; `SegmentedControl(buttons: List<SegmentedControlButtonData>, …)`; `VerticallyScrollableContainer`; `InlineWarningBanner`; `AllIconsKeys.Actions.Attach`, `Webreferences.Server`, `General.Close`, and `AllIconsKeys.Nodes.Folder` and `Nodes.Plugin` (both checked with `javap` on the 2026.1.5 `intellij.platform.jewel.ui.jar` while writing this plan: spec 11 item 7 is settled). For spec 17 also `AllIconsKeys.Nodes.Console`, `General.Web` and `General.Settings`, and `MenuScope.selectableItem(selected, iconKey, keybinding: Set<String>?, onClick, enabled, content)`, whose `keybinding` slot draws a row's shortcut text (all checked with `javap` on the same jar while extending this plan); Jewel 2026.1.5 has no switch (its jar has `Checkbox` and `ToggleableIconActionButton` only), so D15 draws one. Check any other API with `javap` before use. Theme tokens only, except `TandemColors`.
- Fixtures: `name:line` is the 1-based line of `src/test/resources/fixtures/<name>.ndjson`; `transcripts/<name>.jsonl` is its on-disk twin. In tests, `at(name, n)` (`FixtureDecodeTest`) and `fixtureLine(name, n)` (`chat/Replay.kt`) decode one line; `ControlPayloads.x((at(name, n) as CliMessage.ControlResponse).payload)` decodes an answer; `b17` below is `ControlPayloads.contextUsage(…parity-context:17…)!!`, `u16`/`u17` are `ControlPayloads.usage(…parity-usage:16/17…)!!`. The redactor made every path `<path>` and every id a placeholder (`<uuid-28>`).
- Test names are the spec's (9.1–9.4, and 9.7's where it names one; the plan names the rest), verbatim under the Kotlin name rule: Kotlin/JVM rejects `.` `;` `[` `]` `/` `<` `>` `:` `\` in backtick names, so the Kotlin name replaces `: ` with ` - `, drops a leading `/` (`/usage` → `usage`), drops `<`/`>` and `"`, turns `; ` into `, `, and a `// spec: <verbatim name>` comment sits above the test. Names below are already rewritten. New classes use JUnit 5; additions to the JUnit 4 classes (`FixtureDecodeTest`, `HostMessagesTest`, `RobustnessTest`, `ReducerTest`, `ClaudeSessionTest`) keep JUnit 4. No `kotlinx-coroutines-test` on the classpath: coroutine tests run under `runBlocking { withTimeout(10_000) { … } }` with an injected dispatcher, as `McpPanelControllerTest` does.
- Exact values (spec 3, 4): timeouts `get_context_usage` 30 s, `get_usage` with `skip_behaviors` 15 s and without 60 s (`ClaudeSession.mcpRequest` seconds, scaled by `mcpMillisPerSecond` in tests); frames of probes 3.2: `{"type":"control_request","request_id":"c1","request":{"subtype":"get_context_usage"}}`, `{…"request_id":"u1","request":{"subtype":"get_usage","skip_behaviors":true}}`, `{…"request_id":"u2","request":{"subtype":"get_usage"}}`; argv `--add-dir <dir>` per folder after `--resume <id>` / `--fork-session` / `--session-id <id>`; popover width `min(360 dp, chat column − 32 dp)`, at least 280 dp, height at most 60 % of the tool window, 8 dp above the ring (`AboveAnchor(alignEnd = true)`); bar 4 dp, track `dim` at 30 % alpha, a non-zero segment at least 1 px; swatch 8 dp; at most 100 items per group then `and N more`; card frame 6 dp radius, 12 dp padding; share bar 4 dp; pill `×` 16 dp; every clickable row ≥ 24 dp from its top-left (the Driver clicks at `Point(10,10)`); plugin version check `^[0-9A-Za-z][0-9A-Za-z.+-]{0,31}$`; every user-facing string is spec 3.6's, verbatim. Context tones (`TandemColors.contextTone`, light / dark): `MESSAGES` `0xFF3574F0` / `0xFF548AF7`, `MCP_TOOLS` `0xFFE06C4C` / `0xFFF08A6C`, `MEMORY_FILES` `0xFF4FA562` / `0xFF5FB865`, `SYSTEM_PROMPT` `0xFFD9A343` / `0xFFE5B85C`; `NEUTRAL` is the chat palette's `dim`, `FREE` the track (hollow swatch).
- Fixtures of spec 12 and 13 (D8 to D12b): in `queue-fold`, `queue-later` and `queue-cancel` the turn's prompt and the message written while it runs are both `<command_uuid-N>` placeholders: `queue-fold` P `<command_uuid-4>`, F `<command_uuid-25>`; `queue-later` P `<command_uuid-4>`, F `<command_uuid-25>`; `queue-cancel` P1 `<command_uuid-4>`, F1 `<command_uuid-29>`, P2 `<command_uuid-47>`, F2 `<command_uuid-68>`; F's text in `queue-fold` and `queue-later` is `Also say the word KIWI at the end.` (`transcripts/queue-fold.jsonl:23`, `transcripts/queue-later.jsonl:32`), and F1 and F2 never reach a transcript, so tests give them their own text. In `fork-at`, A is `<session_id-2>` (the origin, inits `:6`, `:19`, `:45`), B `<session_id-70>` (the fork, init `:60`), and the cut `<uuid-14>` is the first turn's reply (`fork-at:11`, `transcripts/fork-at-origin.jsonl:20`, `transcripts/fork-at.jsonl:17`). Probe runs (spec 12, 13: **probe run**) are not fixtures: a test never cites one.
- Exact values (spec 12 to 16): frames `{"type":"user","session_id":"","message":{…},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"uuid":"u-1","priority":"later"}` (`HostMessagesTest`'s `user text message` frame with `"priority":"later"` after `uuid`), `{"type":"control_request","request_id":"a1","request":{"subtype":"interrupt","cancel_queued":true}}`, `{"type":"control_request","request_id":"c1","request":{"subtype":"cancel_async_message","message_uuid":"u-1"}}`; `cancel_async_message` 5 s (`mcpRequest` seconds); the capabilities `msg_lifecycle_v1` and `interrupt_cancel_queued_v1`; `<first line>` cut at 60 characters with `…`; the fork argv tail `--resume <A> --fork-session --session-id <B> --resume-session-at <uuid>`, then `--add-dir <dir>` per folder; action id `Tandem.CyclePermissionMode`, `first-keystroke="shift TAB"` in `$default`; the orbs: 0.1.2's as shipped, not changed (`OrbIndicator`'s 12 s cycle and `STILL`, `Spark`'s square one line of the row's text high, in `TandemColors.spark` while running (also under Reduce motion), `text.warning` while waiting and `text.error` while not responding, `withFrameMillis` then `delay(33)`); the shimmer: first sweep at 600 ms, 900 ms eased `(1 − cos πp) / 2`, one every 3000 ms, a 33 ms pause between a sweep's frames (`SHIMMER_FRAME_MS`), band half-width 32 dp, base `text.info`, highlight `JewelTheme.globalColors.text.normal`; the usage card: a 4 dp cost-share bar, `<1%` above 0 and under 0.5 %, `>99%` from 99.5 %, three tips before the disclosure. Strings, verbatim from the spec (hard rule 8: none says "Claude Code"): 12.1 `Sent · Claude reads it at its next step`, `Queued · sends when Claude is done`, `Waits for the shell command's output to go out`, `Withdrawing…`, `Sent while Claude worked`, `↵ to send, ⌘↵ to queue for later`, `Enter to send, Ctrl+Enter to queue for later`, `Claude is working · Esc to stop`, `Type / for commands`, `Send now · ⌘↵ queues it for after this turn`, `Send now · Ctrl+Enter queues it for after this turn`; 12.2 `Claude is still starting: send again in a moment.`, `This claude CLI does not take messages while it works: wait for the reply, or press Esc to stop.`, `Answer the card above first, then send.`, `Slash commands run when Claude is done: wait for the reply, or press Esc to stop.` (only without D8's **after the turn** outcome for `queue-later-command`), `Images go out when Claude is done: wait for the reply, or press Esc to stop.` (only without D8's **runs** outcome for its image message); 12.3 `Edit queued message`, `Cancel queued message`, `Claude had already taken "<first line>"; it was not withdrawn.`, `Not sent: "<first line>". It is back in the composer.`; 12.4 `Shell commands run when Claude is done: wait for the reply, or press Esc to stop.`; 12.5 `Not sent: "<first line>": the CLI ended before reading it. It is back in the composer.`; 13.1 `Fork from here`, `Starts a new session from this point. This one stays as it is.`, `Wait for the reply to finish`, `This reply cannot be forked: the CLI sent no id for it`, `This session has no transcript to fork yet`; 14.1 `Cycle Permission Mode`, `Switch the Tandem chat to the next permission mode: Manual, Accept edits, Plan, then Auto when the model supports it`, `<shortcut> to cycle`, `Permission mode <label>`, `The CLI started in permission mode <mode>, not <mode> as picked.`; 15.1 `The orbs and the shimmer beside Claude's working line stand still.`; 16.2 `<pct>% of cost`, `<1% of cost`, `>99% of cost`, `Web searches <n>`, `<n> requests`, `Tip`, `Tips`, `Show <n> more`, `Show fewer`, `Show session usage`, `Show session usage (after this turn)`.

the Connectors page's `General.Web` (`Browse connectors`), `General.Settings` (`Manage connectors`) and `Webreferences.Server` on every connector row; the drawn switch: a rounded 28 × 16 dp track, the theme's accent colour when on (the checked `Checkbox`'s fill in Jewel's checkbox style; check the token with `javap` and say which in D15's commit body) and `JewelTheme.globalColors.borders.normal` when off, a 12 dp knob in the panel background, drawn at 40 % alpha while not switchable; `CONNECTORS_URL = "https://claude.ai/settings/connectors"` (unverified, spec 11 item 19); `SYNCED_SKILLS_NAMESPACE = "anthropic-skills"`; action `Tandem.AddFilesOrPhotos`, text `Add Files or Photos`, description `Attach images to the Tandem prompt`, `<keyboard-shortcut keymap="$default" first-keystroke="control alt shift O"/>` (⌘⌥⇧O through the macOS keymaps); action `Tandem.NewSession`, text `New Session`, description `Start a new Tandem session with the last model, effort and permission mode`, no shortcut; a tab's label `New session` while the title equals the session id, else the title cut at 32 characters with `…` (32 characters, then `…`, only when longer); its tooltip `<title> · <status word>`, then ` · needs you` with attention (a new session's tooltip starts `New session`); status words `running`, `waiting for permission`, `idle`, `stopped` (the sidebar's); the tab icon 12 × 12 (`JBUI.scale`), the dot 8 (the sidebar's `StatusDot`) centred in `JBUI.CurrentTheme.IconBadge.INFORMATION` (running), `WARNING` (waiting), `SUCCESS` (idle) or `JBUI.CurrentTheme.Label.disabledForeground()` (stopped), with attention a 1 px ring (`JBUI.scale`) at the icon's edge in `IconBadge.WARNING`; the placeholder content's name empty, its panel's hint today's `Create a session with + or open the sidebar.` Strings, verbatim from spec 3.6 (none says "Claude Code"): `‹ Connectors`, `Browse connectors`, `Manage connectors`, `No claude.ai connectors in this session.`, `Last known: the session is not running.`, `Skills`, `Commands`, `No commands or skills`, `Add Files or Photos`, `Attach images to the Tandem prompt`, `New session`, `New Session`, `Start a new Tandem session with the last model, effort and permission mode`, ` · needs you`, the accessibility names `<name> connector` with `On` or `Off`, `<label> plugin`, `/<plugin>:<name>`; reused as they are, never copied into a second constant: the MCP panel's `Pending`, `Needs auth`, `Failed`, `Enabling…`, `Disabling…`, `Could not list servers: <error>`, `The CLI did not answer within 15 s.`, `<name>: <error> · <name> is no longer configured` and its start notices, and the sidebar's status words.
- Personal data: the owner's reference frames come from a work machine; no name, path, company, URL, account, cost or number from them goes into a committed file (this plan, code, tests, docs, commit messages). The UI is described generically, the values in tests are the committed fixtures'. The owner's screenshots of 0.2.0 and of the desktop's `+` menu show connector, plugin, skill and session names from their employer: tests use the fixtures' names (`probe-kit`, `echo`) and made-up generic ones (`claude.ai Alpha`, `plugin-a`, `kit`), never a name from a screenshot.
- Commits: Conventional Commits, staged by path, no trailers, no AI attribution: `git -c user.name="Dago Kogos" -c user.email="34120174+daarko10@users.noreply.github.com" commit --author="Dago Kogos <34120174+daarko10@users.noreply.github.com>" -m "<type>: <subject>"` (written `COMMIT -m …` in the steps below).
- Deviations from the spec (not contract breaks):
  1. Spec 9.4 says `runTest`; the controller test runs under `runBlocking` with the dispatcher injected (no `kotlinx-coroutines-test` beside the platform's fork).
  2. Spec 9.1's `SessionConfigTest` row goes into `ClaudeSessionTest` beside `argv for a new session and for a resumed fork` (no `SessionConfigTest` class exists); spec 9.2's `SessionHandleTest` folder tests go into `SessionManagerTest`, since they need spawns through the fake and its `__spawn__` argv.
  3. Spec 10's D2 is two tasks (D2a requests, reducer and fake; D2b folders) and D6 two (D6a the menu; D6b Add folder), each green with one commit and its own reviewer, so that D0's recording holds back only the folder work.
  4. `NoticeRow` gains `sourceUuid` beside spec 4.3's `usage` and `markdown` (the card's `Retry` needs the uuid); `ContextBreakdownController` takes `home` and `projectRoot` (for `displayPath`) and names its state flow `fetch` (its constructor already has the session's `status`); `chat/` gains `displayPath(…)`, the loading constants `USAGE_LOADING`, `HISTORY_LOADING`, `WINDOW_EMPTY`, and `popoverStatus(…)` with `BREAKDOWN_LOADING`, `BREAKDOWN_UPDATING`, `BREAKDOWN_STOPPED` (the popover's status line), so the strings and the loading, error and stopped states are tested without Compose.
  5. One predicate decides the card and the fetch: `CliMessage.Assistant.isUsageLine()` = `localCommand?.command == "usage" && parentToolUseId == null && uuid != null`, used by the reducer and by `ClaudeSession.readLoop`, so no card waits for a fetch that never starts.
  6. A usage part still `Loading` turns `Failed(Ended)` on `SessionEvent.Ended` and in `settled()` (a stop by the cap or a folder respawn), so it reads `The session has ended.` (spec 3.2, 7) even when the answer can no longer reach the handle.
  7. `ChatRowsBuilder` reopens a cached (frozen) turn whose usage notice changed instance, so a card in an older turn still updates (spec 8 "recomposed only when its part states change" needs it; Review Focus 1).
  8. Not a deviation any more but a spec change: spec 3.3 and 3.5 now give the keyboard of the popover and the Plugins pages (Enter, Space or a click toggles a group or opens a plugin's page; in the `+` menu → on page 1 opens `Plugins`, its only `›` row, and does nothing elsewhere, ← goes back one page everywhere; in the popover → and ← are not bound), with Jewel's reason: its menu does not expose its keyboard selection to the AWT key dispatch the footer menus use, so an arrow key cannot know the selected row. Reviewers check the keys against spec 3.5.
  9. Fake rule (j) keys on the fixture name `parity-*` (their control responses answer requests the host sends out of the recorded order); a new rule (l) rewrites init's `additional_directories` from the fake's own `--add-dir` arguments (minus `TANDEM_FAKE_SKIP_ADD_DIR`), so the confirmation of spec 3.4 is testable; rule (h) serves the first full answer, which is `parity-context:2` (the pre-turn one: `messages` 10 tokens, `totalTokens` 30909, eight categories, `mcpTools` empty, so its groups are `Memory files` and `Skills` alone).
  10. IDE test 2 sends a first prompt and then `/usage` (the fixture's first turn is a model reply); IDE test 4 runs on `plain-reply` twice with rule (l), not on D0's fixture.
  11. The folder checks live in `ide/FolderCheck.kt` (`checkFolder`): file-system checks of the IDE's chooser result; `TandemSessions` exposes `projectDirectory`.
  12. The fake's queue rule is (m), not spec 9.7's (l): this plan gave (l) to `--add-dir` (deviation 9, D2b). D10 writes (m) and one knob spec 9.7 does not name, `TANDEM_FAKE_QUEUED_DELAY_MS` (a sleep after a `queued` lifecycle line that bound a host frame): without it the fake replays a steer's `queued` and `started` lines within milliseconds and IDE test 1 could not see the pending bubble.
  13. D0's run gains a process (spec 10 slice 0, 13.1 "Added folders"): a fork at the first turn's reply with `--add-dir`, third of five, so the plain fork becomes the fourth process and the removal the fifth. `copy_transcript.py` then sees three session ids, and today it refuses more than two: it now copies the first id's transcript as the origin and the last's as the twin, and names the ids between on stderr without copying them. D0 runs after D8 and uses its `fork_at_argv` and `reply_uuid` (spec 10 runs them side by side, but both edit the same scripts).
  14. Spec 10's D12 and D13 are two tasks each (a: the session layer; b: chat and ui), as D2 and D6 are (deviation 3), so the session work sits in the chain's session block: D12a and D13a are additive and green on their own, and D12b removes `latestReply` and `NOT_LATEST` together with their only readers.
  15. Spec 9.7's `SessionConfigTest` row goes into `ClaudeSessionTest` (deviation 2's reason); its `SessionHandleTest` row `a fork at a reply passes its parent's folders after the fork tail` goes into D2b's `SessionManagerTest` (it needs the fake's `__spawn__` argv, and `fork()` copies folders only from D2b on), marked (fork at) and dropped when D0's fork at a reply is **not listed**.
  16. `ChatState.cliCapabilities` is `Set<String>?`, null until the live process's `system/init` (spec 12.6 has `Set<String>`, empty by default), so the composer tells `Claude is still starting…` (no init yet) from `This claude CLI does not take messages while it works…` (an init without both capabilities), spec 12.2's two refusals.
  17. IDE test 4 (spec 9.7) presses Shift+Tab before the first prompt instead of holding the replies with `TANDEM_FAKE_GATE_FILE`, which holds only the CLI's own `control_request`s (the fake's docstring): before the first `user` frame the fake answers no `set_permission_mode` (its first `control_response` after `initialize`, `permission-mode:17`, is replayed only after the first turn), so both presses read Tandem's own picks.
  18. Pure helpers beyond spec 12.7 and 16.3, so their strings are tested without Compose: `chat/QueuedSends.kt` `sendNowTooltip(mac)` and `FOLDED_CAPTION`; `chat/UsageCardModel.kt` `TIPS_SHOWN`, `tipsTitle(count)`, `tipsToggle(count, open)`; `session/` `firstLine(text)` (spec 12.5's 60-character cut, used by the reducer's notices). `PaletteOutcome.Send` gains `later` and `pick` gains `midTurn` (spec 12.1: a palette pick that would send while busy sends it `later`).
  19. A drawn-motion test is required in D14 (Review Focus 10), timeboxed: first `MotionPixelsTest`, which renders the composed row's two moving parts, 0.1.2's orbs (`Spark`, unchanged) and the shimmer's band, headlessly with Compose's `ImageComposeScene` at two frame times (check with `javap` that the class is in the 2026.1.5 platform's bundled Compose jars on the test classpath, and with one try that its effects run on its frame clock and that `Spark` composes there with Jewel's `LocalTextStyle` provided: a few minutes at most); the orbs' frames themselves are 0.1.2's `OrbIndicatorTest`'s, so it only proves that the composed row draws both moving, and both still under `Reduce motion`; only if it cannot run, IDE test 5 (optional in spec 9.7) becomes required: three screenshots 700 ms apart, each cropped to the orbs' square at the top-left of the base's `workingRow("Claude is working")` node (Compose nodes report 0x0 bounds but their real top-left, which the base's `click(Point(10, 10))` relies on: `ToolWindowIdeTest.kt` at `e6e8fee`), a square one row high and no wider than `Spark` (`lineHeight(smallTextStyle())`, in pixels at xvfb's scale of 1), so the label and its band stay outside it, must not all be equal; if neither works, D14's commit body, Review Focus 10's reviewer and D7's PR notes say plainly that the tests pin the shimmer's frame hook and the orbs' frames only, and that the reviewer's reading of `Shimmer.kt`, `WorkingRowItem`, `ThinkingRowItem` and `ToolLine` and the owner's `runIde` pass are the gate.
  20. launch.md tactic 7 ("Protocol work is not done until `cli-contract.yml` covers it"): D7 adds `queue-fold`, `queue-later`, `queue-cancel`, `fork-at` and D8's `queue-cancel-steer`, `queue-during-card` and `parity-requests` recordings to the contract job, with `ContractTest` cases that decode their payloads through `ControlPayloads.contextUsage` and `usage`, `interruptReceipt` and `cancelAsyncAnswer` and assert the outcomes this round switches on (the `parity-*` fixtures came from a scratch wrapper, so D8 scripts their requests as `parity-requests`).
  21. Spec 12.5's IDE notification (beside attention) lives in `ide/SessionNotifier`: D10 changes it and adds a `SessionNotifierTest` case spec 9.7 does not name.
  22. Spec 10 runs D15 and D16 after D14, as here, but this plan's D2b and D6b come last (they wait on D0), so D15 and D16 run before them: D15's IDE test 1 and D6a's keyboard test list page 1 without `Add folder`, and D6b adds it to both (in D0's branch C both keep four rows). Spec 10's D6 work of 17.1 and 17.3 (page 1's icons, the Plugins pages by source) is D6a's, `Add folder`'s icon D6b's; spec 10's D3 work of 17.5 is D3's; `init.skills` is D1's and `ChatState.skills` D2a's.
  23. → is unbound in the `+` menu on every page (spec 3.5 since 17.1: page 1 has two `›` rows): `plusMenuTurn` turns pages back with ← only, and the plan's earlier → on page 0 is gone. The menu's pages are numbered `PLUS_MAIN = 0`, `PLUS_PLUGINS = 1`, `PLUS_CONNECTORS = 2` (from D15) and `PLUS_FIRST_PLUGIN + i` for plugin `i`, `PLUS_FIRST_PLUGIN = 3`.
  24. `chat/ConnectorMenu.kt`'s `McpBusyView` carries the action beside spec 17.5's name and caption, `McpBusyView(name, action, caption)` (`McpAction` is in `chat/` already), so a switch shows its new position while its request runs (spec 17.2); `toggleAction(row)` maps a row to the `McpAction` its switch runs, so the page holds no rule of its own.
  25. Tabs' plumbing (spec 18.4): `SessionTabsController` takes a `TabActions` interface (`show`, `close`), which `SessionActions` implements, and a `focus: (SessionId) -> Unit`, in place of `SessionActions` itself and its own `focusRequests`, so its test records calls without a project's manager; a light project service, `ui/TandemWindowService`, owns the one `SessionActions` of the tool window and its focus requests (which `TandemToolWindow` collects into `sessionShown`), and gives the registered `Tandem.NewSession` action, which has no other way to reach the tool window's state, `newSession()`; `SessionActions.tabs` reads the tab ids from the manager's current values through `sessionTabs`, not from the controller.
  26. Spec 9.8's `a process that ends by itself raises attention unless selected` says the fake exits at its fixture's end; today's fake waits for the host to close stdin. D16 adds the knob `TANDEM_FAKE_EXIT_AT_END=<ms>`: after the last replayed line, sleep that long, then exit 0 (unset: as today), so a test can clear the turn's own attention before the exit.
  27. Spec 18.4 marks the stops Tandem makes; D16 counts the other side instead, by construction the same set: a process end counts only on the path where the process's own exit lets it go (`SessionHandle`'s `process.running.join()` path, when `releaseLocked` finds it still attached), and every stop Tandem makes (`stop`, `stopIfIdle` (the cap and a folder respawn), `close`, a fork's stop, `handOver` and a takeover, `failToStart`) lets the process go first, so it never counts.
  28. D15's IDE test 1 expects the row's shortcut text as the IDE draws it: `Ctrl+Alt+Shift+O` on Linux and Windows, `⌥⇧⌘O` on macOS (`SystemInfo.isMac` in the test); if the macOS job's screenshot shows another order, the literal follows the screenshot.

## Review Focus

1. A usage card in a turn that has since been cached (the user sent the next prompt while the up-to-60 s history scan ran, or pressed `Retry` on an older card) must still update, never show `Reading this machine's sessions…` forever → D3 `ChatRowsBuilderTest` `a usage card in an older turn updates when its answer arrives`, `a usage card in an earlier frozen turn updates and later turns keep their rows` (the case deviation 7 exists for: the card's turn is neither the last frozen turn nor ended by the card) and `Retry on an older card shows it loading again`.
2. A card part still loading when its process ends, is closed, or is stopped by the live-session cap (an idle session during the scan is the cap's first pick) or by a folder respawn must read `The session has ended.` → D2a `ReducerTest` `a process end turns loading usage parts into ended` and `a stop settles loading usage parts`; D2a `ClaudeSessionTest` `the history request is not sent once the process ended`.
3. A memory-file path that only shares a prefix with the home or the project root (`/home/al` and `/home/alex/…`, `/p/app` and `/p/app2/…`) must stay absolute, never read `~ex/…` or `2/…` → D3 `ContextBreakdownModelTest` `a path that only shares a prefix with home or the project stays absolute`.
4. A plugin whose name prefixes another's (`kit` and `kit-pro`) must list only its own commands, and a catalogue entry with nothing after the colon is in no page → D3 `PluginMenuTest` `a plugin whose name prefixes another's lists only its own commands`.
5. A folder added and removed again before the stop must not stop a process for nothing, and a folder path with spaces must stay one argv element → D2b `SessionManagerTest` `removing a folder before the stop leaves the process running`; D1 `ClaudeSessionTest` `added folders become --add-dir after --resume` (its `/b c` entry).
6. A message sent while Claude works is never lost and never dropped in silence: the process ending, a stop, the live-session cap, Stop with `cancel_queued` and a refused withdraw each give it back or say why, and a session with a message waiting is never idle → D10 `ReducerTest` `the process ending returns waiting messages with a WARNING`, `settled returns them with an INFO`, `a refused withdraw puts the message back and says so`, `a refusal after the message started only says so`, `queue-cancel - Stop's cancelled lifecycle returns F2 with one INFO notice and no further turn`; `ClaudeSessionTest` `the process ending drops held prompts and returns them`, `a send the interrupt receipt names nowhere comes back with Stop's notice`; `SessionHandleTest` `a session with a message in the CLI's queue is RUNNING and not idle, so stopIfIdle refuses`; D11 `ChatUiStateTest` `restoreReturned puts the texts ahead of the draft and re-attaches the images once`.
7. One CLI turn stays one visual turn with one footer; the prompt's own lifecycle lines and a `later` message's gap between `result` and `started` change nothing the user sees; a held shell escape never races a `later` message → D10 `the turn's own lifecycle lines change nothing`, `queue-fold, replayed with the host's sends - F waits, then joins the turn after the tool result`, `a held escape waits until no queued message remains`, `a prompt sent while a ! command runs goes out after the escape's frames`, `a prompt held behind a ! that sends nothing goes out when it ends`, `a prompt sent while a context-only escape's quiet result is due goes out after it`; D11 `ChatRowsBuilderTest` `a folded prompt has its caption and the turn one footer`, `queue-later has two footers`; `ComposerModelTest` `busy counts waiting messages`.
8. `--resume-session-at` never goes out without `--fork-session`, never blank, flag-like or outside the session's own history, and the original's transcript stays byte-identical → D9 `ClaudeSessionTest` `a fork at a reply ends with --resume-session-at`; D12a `SessionManagerTest` `fork at a reply resumes with --resume-session-at and starts from the history up to that turn`, `fork at a uuid not in the history gives null and starts nothing`.
9. Shift+Tab is taken only in the composer's text area and only together with Tab's forward move (no keyboard trap), and quick presses step one mode each → D13b `ComposerKeysTest` `Tab moves the focus forward and Shift+Tab is left to the IDE`, `CyclePermissionModeActionTest` `the action is enabled only with the composer's modes`, `the composer offers no modes while a menu is open`, IDE test 4's Tab and its press after a turn; D13a `SessionManagerTest` `two cyclePermissionMode calls with no reply between them send acceptEdits then plan`, `a cycle while a turn runs writes its frame at once`; D13b `ModesTest` `next never offers bypass or dontAsk`.
10. The orbs and the shimmer cost nothing while nothing runs and never change the row's text or semantics per frame → D14 `WorkingIndicatorTest` `waiting and not responding neither animate nor shimmer`, `under reduced motion nothing animates or shimmers`; and they do move on screen while a turn runs, the owner's first check. 0.1.1's bug class was a pure model that said animated while the IDE drew a still glyph, and `754d1eb` pinned it with the composed `WorkingRowItemTest`; 0.1.2's `OrbIndicatorTest` pins the orbs' frames; here the pin is what the composed row draws, since a row that stopped drawing or moving the orbs, or a `Modifier.shimmer` that draws nothing, would pass every state test → D14 `MotionPixelsTest` `the orbs and the band move in the drawn row`, `under Reduce motion the drawn row stands still` (or, per deviation 19, IDE test 5, or the plain statement that only the shimmer's frame hook and the orbs' frames (`OrbIndicatorTest`) are pinned); `WorkingRowItemTest` `the composed shimmer sweeps`, `under Reduce motion the composed shimmer stays at rest`, `the thinking row shimmers only while it streams` pin the shimmer's states as composed, beside the base's tests of the orbs' `animated`; `MotionLoopsTest` checks the shimmer loop's timing and its frame rate (`the shimmer loop draws at most about 30 frames a second`). The reviewer reads `Shimmer.kt` for state read only in draw, a loop that parks in `withFrameMillis` or `delay` and a sweep that pauses `SHIMMER_FRAME_MS` (33 ms) between frames, checks that `WorkingRowItem` passes `view.shimmerEnd > 0` and `ThinkingRowItem` passes `thinkingShimmer(row)`, and that the task's diff leaves `OrbIndicator.kt`, `Spark.kt` and `OrbIndicatorTest.kt` untouched (0.1.2's, approved by the owner).
11. The folder chooser never opens inside the Compose click or in a coroutine launched from composition (the 0.1.1 picture freeze, `6cb1973`) → D6b `FolderChooserTest` `the folder chooser opens once on the EDT after the menu's event, and never for a closed project`; the reviewer reads `PromptBar`'s `Add folder` row for `chooseLater` and for no `choose(` inside a `scope.launch`.
12. Every D8 outcome the code switches on is pinned by a content test written in that outcome's branch, so a CLI drift turns a test red instead of leaving a hand-read report wrong (hard rule 6) → D11 `FixtureDecodeTest` `queue-steer-text - …`, `queue-cancel-steer - …`, `queue-later-command - …`, `queue-during-card - …` and `ReducerTest` `queue-cancel-steer, replayed with the host's sends - …`, `queue-during-card, replayed with the host's sends - …`; D12b `FixtureDecodeTest` `fork-at-live - …`, `fork-at-busy - …` and their transcript cases; D7 Step 5b's contract run of `queue-cancel-steer` and `queue-during-card`.
13. (spec 18) A switch between session tabs keeps every session's draft, opened rows and scroll, as a sidebar click does: one shared Compose panel under every tab, never rebuilt, never disposed by a tab's removal → D16 `SessionTabsControllerTest` `every content's component is the one panel`, `no content's removal disposes the panel`; IDE test `session tabs follow the sessions` (a draft typed in the second tab is still there after Alt+← and Alt+→). The reviewer reads the factory for `isDisposeOnRemove = false`, `setShouldDisposeContent(false)` and the panel's one `dispose()` with the tool window.
14. (spec 18) A session never leaves the tab strip in silence: a CLI that exits, crashes or is killed in a session not shown keeps its tab with `needs you`, and Tandem's own stops raise nothing → D16 `SessionManagerTest` `a process that ends by itself raises attention unless selected`, `the stops Tandem makes raise none`; `SessionTabsTest` `kept while live, selected or with attention, dropped when stopped otherwise`.
15. (spec 18) Tab selection and the manager's selection never call each other in a loop, the platform never removes a tab under a session, and closing or deleting the selected session never selects a tab-less stopped one behind the strip → D16 `SessionTabsControllerTest` `a selection from the source selects its content without calling show`, `a platform removeContent is vetoed and calls close once`; `SessionActionsTest` `closing the selected session selects the next tab, else the previous, else none, before the close` and `deleting the selected session selects the next tab, else the previous, else none, before the delete`.
16. (spec 17) A connector's switch never reads on for a connector that does not work, never sends while another MCP action runs (from the menu or the panel), and ends on what the CLI reports → D3 `ConnectorMenuTest` `a status line for pending, needs-auth, failed and an unknown status, none for connected and disabled`, `a busy row shows its caption and its new position, and no row is switchable`, `toggleAction disables an on row and enables an off one, and runs nothing for a row that cannot switch`; the reviewer reads D15's page for `controller.run(toggleAction(row), row.name)` as its only request path and the follow-up list as the only source of `on`.
17. (spec 17) Before the first init, only real plugins become rows: `anthropic-skills:*` and a project's or user's colon-named entry without a parenthesised description prefix never do, a plugin's label is its display name only when all its entries agree, and a description that is only the prefix leaves no second line → D3 `PluginMenuTest` `before an init a prefix counts only with a parenthesised description, and anthropic-skills never`, `the display name labels a plugin only when its entries agree`, `a description that is only the prefix leaves none`.

  ```python
  # scripts/drive_cli.py
  class Cli:

      def user(self, text: str) -> str          # returns user_content's uuid; callers that ignore it are unchanged
      def user_later(self, text: str) -> str    # user_content([text block], priority="later")
  def reply_uuid(lines: list[str]) -> str | None  # the uuid of the last `assistant` stdout line with parent_tool_use_id null, or None
  def fork_at_argv(extra: list[str], origin: str, fork_id: str, cut: str) -> list[str]
      # extra + ["--resume", origin, "--fork-session", "--session-id", fork_id, "--resume-session-at", cut]

  def queue_fold(cli: Cli) -> None; def queue_later(cli: Cli) -> None; def queue_cancel(cli: Cli) -> None
  def queue_steer_text(cli: Cli) -> None; def queue_cancel_steer(cli: Cli) -> None; def queue_later_command(cli: Cli) -> None
  def queue_during_card(cli: Cli) -> None; def parity_requests(cli: Cli) -> None

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `Cli.__init__` gains `safe_mode: bool = True` (`--safe-mode` in the argv only when true; every other scenario unchanged). `add_dir_spawn(cli: Cli, dirs: list[str]) -> None` in `drive_cli.py`, `add-dir-spawn` in `SCENARIOS`: `main` gives this scenario two new empty directories `dirs = [A, B]` from `tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="tandem-add-dir-")` (outside the run's cwd), starts the first `Cli` with `extra + SCENARIO_FLAGS["add-dir-spawn"] + ["--add-dir", A]` (A last; `SCENARIO_FLAGS["add-dir-spawn"] = ["--setting-sources", "project,local", "--strict-mcp-config"]`) and `safe_mode=False`, runs `add_dir_spawn(cli, dirs)` after its `INITIALIZE`, and removes both directories in its `finally`. Inside, `base = cli.extra[:-2]` is the run's flags without that first `["--add-dir", A]` (the function raises unless `cli.extra[-2:] == ["--add-dir", A]`), and every later process is built from `base`, never from `cli.extra`. Every process of this scenario runs with `safe_mode=False`, as the probes ran (probes "How to read": the argv minus `--safe-mode`) and as Tandem's `SessionConfig.argv` spawns, and with the probes' `--setting-sources project,local --strict-mcp-config` (in `base`, so every later process has them too): no user setting, hook, plugin or MCP server of the recording machine reaches the public fixture or changes the outcome, which then depends on the `--add-dir` flags alone. The scenario, each later process with `first_id` = the previous one's `next_id` and its lines appended to `cli.lines`, as `fork` does: (1) `cli.user("Reply with the single word: one")`, the init's `session_id` (`sid`), `until(is_result)`, `close()`; (2) `Cli(base + ["--resume", sid, "--add-dir", A, "--add-dir", B], …, safe_mode=False)`, `call(INITIALIZE)`, `user("Reply with the single word: two")`, `until(is_result)`, close; (3) (spec 13) a fork at the first turn's reply in spec 13.1's argv: `cut = reply_uuid(<process 1's lines>)` (raise when None), `fork_at_id = str(uuid.uuid4())`, `Cli(fork_at_argv(base, sid, fork_at_id, cut) + ["--add-dir", A], …, safe_mode=False)`, `call(INITIALIZE)`, `user("Reply with the single word: three")`, the init's `session_id` must equal `fork_at_id` (else `RuntimeError`), `until(is_result)`, close (spec 13.1 "Added folders": a fork at a reply passes its parent's folders after the fork tail); (4) a fork in Tandem's own argv (`SessionSpawner.configFor`, D1's argv test): `fork_id = str(uuid.uuid4())`, `Cli(base + ["--resume", sid, "--fork-session", "--session-id", fork_id, "--add-dir", A], …, safe_mode=False)`, `call(INITIALIZE)`, `user("Reply with the single word: four")`, the init's `session_id` must equal `fork_id` (else `RuntimeError`, as `ask_two` fails a run that did not ask), `until(is_result)`, close (spec 3.4 and 7 pass `--add-dir` on a fork spawn: this records that exact argv); (5) the removal: `Cli(base + ["--resume", sid, "--add-dir", B], …, safe_mode=False)` (A left out), `call(INITIALIZE)`, `user("Reply with the single word: five")`, `until(is_result)`, close (spec 3.4: does a folder passed before leave with a `--resume` without it).   //   state as sent (queued, started, completed, cancelled; any other string kept); a missing or non-string command_uuid or state → Unknown(raw)
  // protocol/QueueReplies.kt
  data class InterruptReceipt(val stillQueued: List<String>, val cancelled: List<String>)   // an absent or mistyped list reads as empty; a non-string entry is skipped
  fun interruptReceipt(payload: JsonObject?): InterruptReceipt
  fun cancelAsyncAnswer(payload: JsonObject?): Boolean?                                    // `cancelled`; null when absent or not a boolean
  // session/ClaudeSession.kt
  SessionConfig(+ resumeSessionAt: String? = null)
  //   argv(): "--resume-session-at", <uuid> right after "--session-id", before D1's "--add-dir" entries
  //   init: require(resumeSessionAt == null || forkSession) { "--resume-session-at only with --fork-session: without it the CLI branches the original's transcript." }
  //         require(resumeSessionAt == null || (resumeSessionAt.isNotBlank() && !resumeSessionAt.startsWith("-"))) { "A fork point is a message uuid, never a flag." }
  ```
- Wire keys (recorded): `{"type":"command_lifecycle","command_uuid":…,"state":…,"session_id":…,"uuid":…}` (`queue-fold:3`, `:19`, `:22`, `:52`; `queue-cancel:24`, `:55`); the `cancel_async_message` answer `{"cancelled":true}` (`queue-cancel:25`); the `interrupt` receipt `{"still_queued":[],"cancelled":["<command_uuid-68>"]}` (`queue-cancel:56`). `SystemStatus` and `Result` keep their fields: `system/status`'s `user_message_uuids` (`queue-fold:23`), `result.user_message_uuid` (`CliMessage.Result` has `userMessageUuids` only) and `result.queued_turn_count` are read from `raw` in the tests, not decoded (`queued_turn_count` is never a turn-over signal, spec 12.5). `SystemInit.capabilities` is decoded already. No strict UUID check on `resumeSessionAt` (the fixtures and IDE tests use placeholders such as `<uuid-14>` and `a1`; spec 13.1).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: D1's types, `ControlPayloads.contextUsage` / `usage`, `HostMessages.getContextUsage` / `getUsage`, `SystemInit.plugins` / `additionalDirectories`.
- Produces (spec 4.2):
  ```kotlin
  typealias ControlResult<T> = McpResult<T>; typealias ControlError = McpError            // McpControls.kt; no rename this round
  interface ContextControls { suspend fun contextBreakdown(id: SessionId): ControlResult<ContextBreakdown> }   // ContextControls.kt
  ChatState(+ contextBreakdown: ContextBreakdown? = null, plugins: List<PluginInfo> = emptyList(), additionalDirectories: List<String> = emptyList(),
      skills: List<String> = emptyList())   // skills: spec 17.5
  ChatEntry.Notice(+ sourceUuid: String? = null, usage: UsageCard? = null)            // after runCommand
  data class UsageCard(val session: UsagePartState<SessionUsage>, val history: UsagePartState<UsageHistory?>)
  sealed interface UsagePartState<out T> {
      data object Loading : UsagePartState<Nothing>
      data class Loaded<T>(val value: T) : UsagePartState<T>
      data class Failed(val error: ControlError) : UsagePartState<Nothing>
  }
  enum class UsagePart { SESSION, HISTORY }
  SessionEvent.ContextBreakdownListed(val breakdown: ContextBreakdown)
  SessionEvent.UsageFetched(val uuid: String, val part: UsagePart, val result: ControlResult<UsageAnswer>)
  SessionEvent.UsageRetry(val uuid: String, val part: UsagePart)
  internal fun CliMessage.Assistant.isUsageLine(): Boolean   // localCommand?.command == "usage" && parentToolUseId == null && uuid != null
  class ClaudeSession {
      suspend fun contextUsage(): ControlResult<ContextBreakdown>      // mcpRequest(30, getContextUsage): Ok → dispatch ContextBreakdownListed;
                                                                     // success without categories → Failed(Rejected("the CLI's answer had no categories"))
      suspend fun usage(skipBehaviors: Boolean): ControlResult<UsageAnswer>   // mcpRequest(15 or 60, getUsage); null decode → Failed(Rejected("the CLI's answer had no session"))
      fun retryUsage(uuid: String, part: UsagePart)                   // dispatch UsageRetry, then SESSION: both requests as the fetch does; HISTORY: usage(false) alone
  }
  class SessionManager : McpControls, ContextControls {
      override suspend fun contextBreakdown(id: SessionId): ControlResult<ContextBreakdown>   // NotRunning without a live process; coalesced
      fun retryUsage(id: SessionId, uuid: String, part: UsagePart)   // handle.pick(live = { it.retryUsage(uuid, part) }, stopped = UsageFetched(uuid, part, Failed(NotRunning)))
  }
  ```
- `readLoop` (spec 3.2, 4.2): dispatching a `CliMessage.Assistant` with `isUsageLine()` remembers its uuid (`pendingUsage`, the latest one); the next `CliMessage.Result` (recorded: `local_command` `usage` or `cost`, `parity-usage:23`, `:29`) clears it and launches the fetch for that uuid in the session's scope, so `get_usage` goes out between turns, as the probes sent it: `usage(true)` → `dispatch(UsageFetched(uuid, SESSION, it))`; then, unless the process ended (`ended`), `usage(false)` → `dispatch(UsageFetched(uuid, HISTORY, it))`. A process that ends before that result fetches nothing; deviation 6 settles the card. `TranscriptReader` never fetches.
- Coalescing: make `McpRefresh` generic (`private class Coalesced<T> { var running: Job?; var next: CompletableDeferred<McpResult<T>>? }`) and `startRefreshes` a generic `startCoalesced(handle, entry, first, run: suspend () -> McpResult<T>)`; `refreshMcp` keeps its behaviour (its tests stay green) and `contextBreakdown` gets its own `WeakHashMap<SessionHandle, Coalesced<ContextBreakdown>>`, without `mcpActions`.
- Reducer rules (spec 4.2 plus deviations 5 and 6): `SystemInit` replaces `plugins`, `skills` (spec 17) and `additionalDirectories` (absent means empty). A `CliMessage.Assistant` with `isUsageLine()` goes through `localCommandNotice` with `sourceUuid = uuid` and `usage = UsageCard(Loading, Loading)`; every other local-command line and `SessionEvent.LocalCommandOutput` get none. `UsageFetched` finds the notice by `sourceUuid`, scanning back from the end (none → the same state instance, logged at debug with the uuid only), and replaces it: `SESSION` + `Ok` → `Loaded(answer.session)` or `Failed(Rejected("the CLI's answer had no session"))` when null; `HISTORY` + `Ok` → `Loaded(answer.history)`; `Failed(e)` → `Failed(e)`. `UsageRetry(SESSION)` sets both parts `Loading`, `UsageRetry(HISTORY)` the history part. `ContextBreakdownListed` replaces `contextBreakdown`. `SessionEvent.Ended` and `settled()` turn every `Loading` part into `Failed(McpError.Ended)`; `settled()` keeps `contextBreakdown`.
- Fake CLI (spec 9.2, deviation 9), each rule in the docstring's list after (g): **(h)** `get_context_usage` is answered inline, at any time, with the fixture's first `control_response` whose payload has a `categories` array with a `messages` category, else `{"subtype":"error","error":"Unsupported control request subtype: get_context_usage"}`; **(i)** `get_usage` with `skip_behaviors: true` with the first whose payload has a `session` object and `behaviors: null`, without it with the first whose `behaviors` is an object, else the same error naming `get_usage`; **(j)** in a fixture whose name starts with `parity-`, every `control_response` line leaves the in-order replay (`initialize` still answered by (a)), and any other host control request gets a bare success, as in an MCP fixture (c); **(k)** `--add-dir` is already in the `__spawn__` argv. `TANDEM_FAKE_DELAY_MS` delays the (h) and (i) answers too.

**Depends on:** D9, D2a (`Reducer`, `ChatState`, `ClaudeSession`) (Opus; reviewer Opus). Spec 12.1 to 12.6 (the session layer), 9.7 Reducer, reader, session and fake.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/main/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/session/ChatState.kt`, `.../session/Reducer.kt`, `.../session/ClaudeSession.kt`, `.../session/SessionHandle.kt`, `.../session/SessionManager.kt`, `.../session/transcript/TranscriptChain.kt`, `.../session/transcript/TranscriptReader.kt`, `.../ide/SessionNotifier.kt`, `src/test/resources/fake-claude.py` (rule (m), `TANDEM_FAKE_QUEUED_DELAY_MS`, the docstring)
- Test: `src/test/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/session/ReducerTest.kt`, `.../session/ClaudeSessionTest.kt`, `.../session/SessionHandleTest.kt`, `.../session/SessionManagerTest.kt`, `.../session/transcript/TranscriptReaderTest.kt`, `.../ide/SessionNotifierTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: D9 `CommandLifecycle`, `HostMessages.userMessage(…, later)`, `interrupt(…, cancelQueued)`, `cancelAsyncMessage`, `interruptReceipt`, `cancelAsyncAnswer`; `ClaudeSession.mcpRequest` (the MCP round's).
- Produces (spec 12.6; deviation 16):
  ```kotlin
  // session/ChatState.kt
  ChatState(+ pendingSends: List<PendingSend> = emptyList(), returnedSends: List<ReturnedSend> = emptyList(), cliCapabilities: Set<String>? = null)
  data class PendingSend(val uuid: String, val text: String, val images: List<UserImage>, val sentAtMillis: Long, val later: Boolean,
      val stage: PendingStage, val restoreOnWithdraw: Boolean = false)
  enum class PendingStage { HELD, QUEUED, WITHDRAWING }
  data class ReturnedSend(val uuid: String, val text: String, val images: List<UserImage>)
  ChatEntry.User(+ folded: Boolean = false)
  internal fun firstLine(text: String): String   // the text's first line, cut at 60 characters with "…" (spec 12.5)
  internal fun ChatState.forkHistory(): ChatState   // settled() without pendingSends and returnedSends, adding no notice (spec 12.5);
  //   SessionManager.fork builds its history from it, not from settled(), so a waiting message stays the original's and no
  //   "Not sent" notice or returned text reaches the fork
  // session/Reducer.kt
  SessionEvent.UserSent(+ later: Boolean = false, live: Boolean = false, held: Boolean = false, folded: Boolean = false)
  SessionEvent.SendReleased(val uuid: String)                                   // HELD → QUEUED
  SessionEvent.WithdrawRequested(val uuid: String, val restore: Boolean)
  SessionEvent.SendWithdrawn(val uuid: String, val restore: Boolean, val notice: String?)
  SessionEvent.WithdrawRefused(val uuid: String, val reason: String?, val text: String)   // text: the message's, read from its PendingSend when the withdraw starts
  SessionEvent.ReturnedTaken(val uuids: List<String>)
  // session/ClaudeSession.kt
  fun sendUserMessage(text: String, images: List<ImageBlock> = emptyList(), later: Boolean = false)
  fun withdraw(uuid: String, restore: Boolean)
  // session/SessionHandle.kt
  Outbound.Prompt(val text: String, val images: List<ImageBlock> = emptyList(), val later: Boolean = false)
  // session/SessionManager.kt
  fun send(id: SessionId, text: String, images: List<ImageBlock> = emptyList(), later: Boolean = false)
  fun withdraw(id: SessionId, uuid: String, restore: Boolean)   // the live process's withdraw; without one nothing (settled() already returned what waited)
  fun takeReturned(id: SessionId, uuids: List<String>)          // ReturnedTaken on the live process's state or the stopped chat (handle.pick)
  ```
- Notices (spec 12.3, 12.5; `<first line>` is `firstLine(text)`; logs name uuids and stages only): the process ending, WARNING `Not sent: "<first line>": the CLI ended before reading it. It is back in the composer.`; Stop's `cancelled` and `settled()`, INFO `Not sent: "<first line>". It is back in the composer.`; a refused withdraw, INFO `Claude had already taken "<first line>"; it was not withdrawn.`
- Reducer rules (spec 12.6): `SystemInit` sets `cliCapabilities` to its list (an init without the key: empty), `Ended` and `settled()` set it to null. `UserSent(folded = true)` appends `User(folded = true)` and changes nothing else. `UserSent(live = true)` while a turn is open, a message waits, or with `held` appends a `PendingSend` (`HELD` when held, else `QUEUED`; `sentAtMillis` its `atMillis`) and leaves the turn and the entries alone; any other `UserSent` is reduced as today (the reader's replace-the-open-turn rule is kept). `CommandLifecycle`: `started` for a waiting uuid removes it and appends `User(folded = turn != null)`, and with no turn open also opens one (busy, `turnInterrupted = false`, `TurnProgress(uuid, at, …)`), as a send does today; `cancelled` for a waiting uuid removes it: a `WITHDRAWING` one with `restoreOnWithdraw == false` is dropped silently, a `WITHDRAWING` one with it goes to `returnedSends` without a notice, any other goes to `returnedSends` with Stop's INFO notice; `queued`, `completed` and a uuid that is not waiting (the turn's own prompt, `interrupt:5`, `:7`, `:20`) change nothing (the same instance). Before a `result`'s `TurnEnded`, each waiting uuid its `user_message_uuids` names is appended as a folded `User` (the fallback when no lifecycle came). `SendReleased` turns `HELD` into `QUEUED`. `WithdrawRequested` sets `WITHDRAWING` and `restoreOnWithdraw = restore`. `SendWithdrawn` for a waiting uuid removes it and, with `restore`, adds it to `returnedSends` (with `notice` when given); for a uuid no longer waiting it changes nothing (the CLI's `cancelled` line comes before its answer, `queue-cancel:24`, `:25`). `WithdrawRefused` puts a waiting send back to `QUEUED` with the refusal notice; for a uuid no longer waiting (its `started`, or a `result` naming it, came before the answer) it only adds the notice, with the event's text, and changes nothing else. `Ended` returns every waiting message with the WARNING, `settled()` with the INFO; `ReturnedTaken` removes those uuids from `returnedSends`.
- `ClaudeSession` (spec 12.3, 12.4): `sendUserMessage` takes the shell lock; while `held` is not empty (a `!` command runs, or escapes or prompts are held), an output frame waits for its quiet `result` (`pendingOutput != null`) or a quiet send's zero-turn `result` is still due (`quietSends` not empty: a context-only escape's frame, MCP 12.9 item 7), the prompt joins `held` and dispatches `UserSent(live = true, held = true, later)`, whatever the capabilities (spec 12.4 rule 2); otherwise it dispatches `UserSent(live = true, later)` and writes `userMessage(…, later)` at once. `heldEscapes` becomes `held: ArrayDeque<Held>` (`private sealed interface Held { class Shell(val id: String); class Escape(…); class Prompt(val uuid: String, val frame: JsonObject) }`). A running `!` command takes its place in `held` when it starts: `event(ShellStarted(id, …))` appends `Held.Shell(id)` (and a `ClaudeSession` whose initial state has a running `Shell` entry starts with its place), because its escape reaches the session only after `SessionManager.runShellJob` has dispatched `ShellEnded` (spec 12.4); `sendShellEscape(id, …)` replaces `Held.Shell(id)` with its `Held.Escape` (appending as today when there is no place), and the new `shellUnsent(id)`, which `runShellJob` calls through the handle's live session on every path that returns without `sendShellFrames` (not started, failed, killed at the IDE's exit, shutting down, closed), removes the place; both then call `advanceShell()`, so a prompt never waits with nothing to release it. `advanceShell` writes head prompts once `pendingOutput == null` and `quietSends` is empty (a `Held.Shell` ahead of them holds them) (dispatching `SendReleased`), and it also runs where today's code releases a held output frame on a quiet `result`, so a prompt held behind a quiet send goes out right after that result, and starts a head escape only when `turn == null` and no `QUEUED` send remains (rule 1: a `later` message starts about 10 ms after the running turn's `result`, `queue-later:51` → `:53`). `withdraw(uuid, restore)`: a held prompt leaves `held` with `SendWithdrawn(uuid, restore, null)`; a uuid not waiting does nothing; otherwise `WithdrawRequested`, then in the session's scope `mcpRequest(5, { cancelAsyncMessage(it, uuid) }) { Ok(cancelAsyncAnswer(it)) }` (5 s, scaled by `mcpMillisPerSecond` in tests): an answer `true` → `SendWithdrawn(uuid, restore, null)`; `false`, null, an error or a timeout → `WithdrawRefused(uuid, the error text or null, the send's text)`. `interrupt()`: held prompts are dropped and returned (`SendWithdrawn(restore = true, notice = Stop's INFO)`); with `"interrupt_cancel_queued_v1"` in `cliCapabilities` and a `QUEUED` send waiting, the frame carries `cancel_queued` and goes through `request()` in the session's scope, each `interruptReceipt(payload).cancelled` uuid → `SendWithdrawn(uuid, restore = true, Stop's INFO)` (a no-op when its `cancelled` line came first, `queue-cancel:55`, `:56`), a `stillQueued` one stays a bubble, and every `QUEUED` send still waiting when the receipt arrives that neither list names → `SendWithdrawn(uuid, restore = true, Stop's INFO)` too (the CLI no longer holds it and no later line would clear it, so Stop always clears what waits; a `WITHDRAWING` one waits for its own answer); otherwise today's frame, byte for byte. The process ending drops `held` (its prompts come back through `Ended`).
- `SessionHandle`: `deliver` passes `later`; `idleLocked` also needs `pendingSends.isEmpty()`; `Snapshot.status` is `RUNNING` while `busy || pendingSends.isNotEmpty()` (`WAITING` first), so the cap and a folder respawn leave it alone (spec 12.5). `SessionManager.watch` raises the turn-end attention only when `turnsEnded` grew and `pendingSends` is empty, and moves its count on either way; `SessionNotifier` does the same for `FINISHED` (deviation 21).
- Reader (spec 12.1 Reload): `TranscriptChain` counts a non-sidechain `attachment` line whose `attachment.type` is `queued_command` and whose `commandMode` is absent or `prompt` as conversational; `TranscriptReader` reads it as `UserSent(uuid = attachment.source_uuid ?: the line's uuid, text = its string prompt or its text blocks joined by "\n", images = its base64 `image` blocks (unrecorded), atMillis = its timestamp, folded = true)`; every other `attachment` stays skipped.
- Fake rule **(m)** (spec 9.7's (l), deviation 12), after (l) in the docstring's list: in a fixture whose name starts with `queue-`, a line naming a `<command_uuid-N>` not yet bound (as `command_uuid` or inside a `user_message_uuids` list) binds it to the oldest host `user` frame read and not yet bound, waiting for the next one when there is none, and every bound placeholder in the line is rewritten to that frame's `uuid`; a `command_lifecycle` `cancelled` line for a placeholder bound after the last `init` (a message sent while the turn ran, not the turn's own prompt) first waits for the host's next `cancel_async_message` or `interrupt`, and the `control_response` after it answers that request (`queue-cancel:24`-`:25`, `:55`-`:56`; the prompt's own `cancelled` at `:61` does not wait); any other replayed `control_response` still pairs with the host's next `control_request`; the wait before an `init` is met by a frame already bound since the last `result`; rule (g) is off for these fixtures. `TANDEM_FAKE_QUEUED_DELAY_MS`: after a `queued` lifecycle line that bound a host frame, sleep this long (unset or 0: no sleep). `--resume-session-at` needs no rule: the fake ignores the flag and logs it in `__spawn__`.
