**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: D9 `SessionConfig.resumeSessionAt`.
- Produces (spec 13.2):
  ```kotlin
  ChatEntry.Assistant(+ lastUuid: String? = null)              // the uuid of its latest complete line; null for a message drafted from deltas only
  internal fun ChatState.forkedAt(uuid: String): ChatState?   // null when no main-thread Assistant entry has uuid in its uuids
  SessionHandle(id, parentScope, initialState, listing, createdAt, forkOf: SessionId? = null, forkAt: String? = null, pendingTitle: String? = null)
  //   internal val forkAt: String? — with forkOf, the line of forkOf's transcript this fork starts after; passed until this session has a transcript
  // SessionSpawner.configFor: forkOf = handle.forkOf.takeUnless { hasTranscript } as today; resumeSessionAt = handle.forkAt.takeIf { forkOf != null }
  sealed interface ForkResult { data class Started(val handle: SessionHandle) : ForkResult; enum class Refused : ForkResult { NO_TRANSCRIPT, NOT_IN_HISTORY, BUSY } }   // SessionManager.kt
  suspend fun forkResult(id: SessionId, atMessage: String? = null): ForkResult   // SessionManager; why nothing forked (BUSY: D12b's refused stop, hard rule 5)
  suspend fun fork(id: SessionId, atMessage: String? = null): SessionHandle?   // (forkResult(id, atMessage) as? ForkResult.Started)?.handle; the sidebar's whole fork and existing tests unchanged
  ```
- Rules: the reducer's complete-assistant-line branch gives a new entry `lastUuid = message.uuid` and copies an existing one with `lastUuid = message.uuid ?: existing.lastUuid`; an entry drafted from stream deltas keeps null. `forkedAt(uuid)`: `forkHistory()` first (D10: `settled()` without the waiting and returned messages and their notices); the first main-thread (`parentToolUseId == null`) `Assistant` whose `uuids` contains `uuid`; the kept entries end before the first `User` or `Shell` entry after it, so the turn and its footer stay; `entryIndex` empty, `context` null (the ring waits for the fork's first turn), `tasks` kept only for calls still shown. `forkResult(id, atMessage)` (`fork` is its wrapper): the source is `id` when it has a transcript, else its `forkOf` at its `forkAt` (a fork with no transcript of its own forks its original at the same line); history and title as today (the open original's `forkHistory()`, D10, else its loaded transcript); `start = if (atMessage == null) history else history.forkedAt(atMessage) ?: return Refused.NOT_IN_HISTORY` (no transcript to fork → `Refused.NO_TRANSCRIPT`, where today's `fork` returns null); `cut = atMessage ?: sourceAt`; `registerLocked(forkId, start.copy(sessionId = null), forkOf = source, forkAt = cut, pendingTitle = "$title (fork)")`, then select and start as today, `Started(fork)`. Logs: `session <id> forked as <id>` and whether at a point; ids only. Tandem never falls back to a whole fork: an argv the CLI refuses (`No message found with message.uuid of: <uuid>`, exit 1, a probe run) shows through today's result and exit paths.

### Task D13a: session-mode-cycle

**Depends on:** D12a (`SessionHandle`, `SessionManager`, `Reducer`) (Opus; reviewer Opus). Spec 14.1 ("No stale reads", "Refused, stopped, busy"), 14.3 (the session part). Additive.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/main/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/session/ChatState.kt` (`rejectedPermissionModes`, `launchPermissionMode`), `.../session/Reducer.kt`, `.../session/ClaudeSession.kt` (the initial state's `launchPermissionMode`), `.../session/SessionHandle.kt` (`pickMode`), `.../session/SessionManager.kt` (`cyclePermissionMode`)
- Test: `src/test/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/session/ReducerTest.kt`, `.../session/ClaudeSessionTest.kt`, `.../session/SessionManagerTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (spec 14.3):
  ```kotlin
  ChatState(+ rejectedPermissionModes: Set<PermissionMode> = emptySet(), launchPermissionMode: PermissionMode? = null)
  //   Reducer: rejectModeChange adds the refused mode; SystemInit and SystemStatus remove the mode they report; Ended and settled() clear it
  //   launchPermissionMode: ClaudeSession's initial state sets it to config.permissionMode; a PermissionModeRequested with a requestId (a
  //   live pick before the first init: Shift+Tab or the menu in a fresh or resumed chat) clears it, since the CLI's answer to that
  //   set_permission_mode already reports a refusal (rejectModeChange); the first SystemInit after the spawn, when it carries a
  //   permissionMode and launchPermissionMode is still set, compares the two and adds the WARNING "The CLI started in permission mode
  //   <init's wire>, not <launch's wire> as picked." when they differ (spec 14.1, hard rule 5); the first SystemInit clears it either way
  //   (one without permissionMode says nothing); Ended clears it too
  internal fun SessionHandle.pickMode(next: (ChatState) -> PermissionMode): PermissionMode   // a member
  //   under the handle's lock: mode = next(liveLocked()?.state?.value ?: the handle's state); pick(live = { it.setPermissionMode(mode) }, stopped = PermissionModeRequested(mode)); mode
  fun cyclePermissionMode(id: SessionId, next: (ChatState) -> PermissionMode): PermissionMode?   // SessionManager; never waits; null for an unknown id
  ```
- Rules: the read happens under the handle's lock from the live process's own state, which holds every earlier pick synchronously (`ClaudeSession.dispatch`), so quick presses and key repeat step one mode each; the lock is never held across I/O (today's `pick`); the pick goes through today's path (`PermissionModeRequested`, `set_permission_mode`); a stopped session records it for the next spawn's `--permission-mode`. Logs: the mode's wire name only.

```kotlin
`an init whose mode differs from the spawn's --permission-mode says so`   // ChatState(launchPermissionMode = AUTO) + the first system/init of permission-mode with "permissionMode":"default" → one WARNING "The CLI started in permission mode default, not auto as picked.", permissionMode DEFAULT, launchPermissionMode null; launchPermissionMode equal to the init's mode → no notice; a second init → no notice
`a pick before the first init cancels the launch-mode check`   // ChatState(launchPermissionMode = ACCEPT_EDITS) + PermissionModeRequested(PLAN, "r1") + its success control_response (request_id "r1") + the first system/init of permission-mode with "permissionMode":"plan" → no WARNING, permissionMode PLAN, launchPermissionMode null; ChatState(launchPermissionMode = ACCEPT_EDITS) + that init with "permissionMode" removed → no WARNING, launchPermissionMode null (the spawn with auto that starts in default stays the test above)
`a refused mode joins rejectedPermissionModes, a reported one leaves, Ended clears them`   // permission-mode-rejected replayed (:17 refuses bypassPermissions, error_code "bypass_not_launched") → rejectedPermissionModes == {BYPASS_PERMISSIONS}, today's ERROR notice unchanged; then a SystemStatus reporting bypassPermissions → empty; {AUTO} + Ended("x", isError = false) → empty; {AUTO}.settled() → empty
// SessionManagerTest (JUnit 5)
`two cyclePermissionMode calls with no reply between them send acceptEdits then plan`   // a scripted live session, no answer written: two calls → ACCEPT_EDITS, then PLAN; the process got two set_permission_mode frames, "acceptEdits" then "plan"
`a stopped session's cycle reaches the next spawn's --permission-mode`   // a stopped session: two calls; send → the spawn's argv holds "--permission-mode", "plan"
`cyclePermissionMode on an unknown id gives null`
`a cycle while a turn runs writes its frame at once`   // a scripted live session with a turn open (a prompt sent, no result written): one cyclePermissionMode call → the process got its set_permission_mode frame before any result, and the turn is still open (spec 14.1: a running turn does not block the cycle)
// ClaudeSessionTest (JUnit 4)
`the spawn's --permission-mode is the launch mode`   // a ClaudeSession with config.permissionMode AUTO → state.value.launchPermissionMode == AUTO; without one → null
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run** `./gradlew test --init-script scratch/central-mirror.init.gradle.kts --tests 'dev.daarkolabs.tandem.session.*'` → compilation errors (`rejectedPermissionModes`, `cyclePermissionMode`).

- Modify: `.../chat/ComposerModel.kt` (`ContextFormat.percentOf`, `ComposerModel` fields), `.../chat/ChatRow.kt` (`NoticeRow`), `.../chat/ChatRowsBuilder.kt`, `src/test/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/chat/Replay.kt` (`replay` gains `builder: ChatRowsBuilder = ChatRowsBuilder(options)`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: D1's types (`SystemInit.skills` among them); D2a's `UsageCard`, `UsagePartState`, `ChatEntry.Notice.sourceUuid/usage`, `ChatState.plugins`, `ChatState.skills`; PR #9's `composerModel(state, known, knownCommands)` and `CommandCatalogue`; the MCP round's `McpPanelModel`, `McpGroupModel`, `McpRowModel`, `McpSource.CLAUDEAI`, `McpAction` (`chat/McpPanelModel.kt`) and `mcpPanel(state, ownServer, loginHinted, live)`.
- Produces (spec 4.3):
  ```kotlin
  // chat/ContextBreakdownModel.kt
  enum class ContextTone { MESSAGES, MCP_TOOLS, MEMORY_FILES, SYSTEM_PROMPT, NEUTRAL, FREE }
  data class CategoryRow(val name: String, val tokens: String, val share: String, val tone: ContextTone, val description: String)
  data class BarSegment(val tone: ContextTone, val fraction: Float)
  data class GroupItem(val label: String, val tokens: String, val dim: Boolean, val tooltip: String?)
  data class BreakdownGroup(val key: String, val title: String, val total: String, val count: Int, val items: ImmutableList<GroupItem>, val more: Int)
  data class ContextPopoverModel(val value: String?, val segments: ImmutableList<BarSegment>, val rows: ImmutableList<CategoryRow>, val groups: ImmutableList<BreakdownGroup>)
  const val GROUP_ITEM_CAP: Int = 100
  data class PopoverStatus(val headerSuffix: String?, val line: String?, val error: Boolean, val dimRows: Boolean)
  const val BREAKDOWN_LOADING = "Loading the breakdown…"; const val BREAKDOWN_UPDATING = " · Updating…"
  const val BREAKDOWN_STOPPED = "The session is not running. The breakdown updates with the next prompt."
  fun popoverStatus(loading: Boolean, failedText: String?, stopped: Boolean, hasRows: Boolean): PopoverStatus
  fun contextPopover(breakdown: ContextBreakdown?, ring: ContextUsage?, home: String?, projectRoot: String?): ContextPopoverModel
  fun displayPath(path: String, home: String?, projectRoot: String?): String
  object ContextFormat { fun percentOf(tokens: Long, max: Long): String }   // a new member of the existing object (chat/ComposerModel.kt)
  // chat/UsageCardModel.kt
  data class UsageFact(val label: String, val value: String)
  data class ModelBlock(val label: String, val cost: String?, val rows: ImmutableList<UsageFact>, val costShare: Float? = null, val costShareText: String? = null)   // costShare: spec 16
  data class BehaviorLine(val pct: String, val label: String, val known: Boolean)   // known: Tandem's wording; false: the CLI's recommendation title
  data class ShareRow(val name: String, val kind: String, val fraction: Float, val pct: String)
  data class UsageWindowModel(val caveat: String, val behaviors: ImmutableList<BehaviorLine>, val shares: ImmutableList<ShareRow>, val tips: ImmutableList<UsageTip>)   // spec 16: every tip, in place of 3.2's first
  sealed interface UsageSection<out T> {
      data object Loading : UsageSection<Nothing>
      data class Shown<T>(val value: T) : UsageSection<T>
      data class Error(val text: String, val retry: Boolean) : UsageSection<Nothing>
      data class Note(val text: String) : UsageSection<Nothing>
  }
  data class UsageCardModel(val facts: UsageSection<ImmutableList<UsageFact>>, val promptCache: String?, val models: ImmutableList<ModelBlock>,
      val history: UsageSection<Pair<UsageWindowModel?, UsageWindowModel?>>, val cliText: String, val cliTextOpen: Boolean)   // a null window: WINDOW_EMPTY
  const val USAGE_LOADING = "Loading usage…"; const val HISTORY_LOADING = "Reading this machine's sessions…"; const val WINDOW_EMPTY = "Nothing recorded for this window."
  fun usageCard(card: UsageCard, noticeText: String, models: List<ModelOption>, activeModel: String?): UsageCardModel
  fun formatCost(usd: Double): String
  fun promptCacheLine(text: String): String?
  fun undrawnCliLines(text: String): Boolean   // a line of the CLI's text the card neither draws nor leaves out on purpose (spec 3.2)
  fun costShare(costUsd: Double?, totalUsd: Double?): Pair<Float, String>?   // spec 16: null without both, or with a total of 0
  const val TIPS_SHOWN: Int = 3                                             // spec 16 (deviation 18)
  fun tipsTitle(count: Int): String                                         // "Tip" for one, "Tips" for several
  fun tipsToggle(count: Int, open: Boolean): String?                        // "Show <count − 3> more" or "Show fewer" past three; else null
  // chat/PluginMenu.kt (spec 17.5's block, which replaces 4.3's)
  enum class PluginItemKind { SKILL, COMMAND }
  data class PluginCommand(val name: String, val command: String, val description: String, val tooltip: String, val kind: PluginItemKind)
  data class PluginEntry(val key: String, val name: String, val label: String, val version: String?, val source: String?,
      val skills: ImmutableList<PluginCommand>, val commands: ImmutableList<PluginCommand>)   // key: the name, made unique as McpRowModel.key ("kit", "kit#2")
  const val SYNCED_SKILLS_NAMESPACE: String = "anthropic-skills"
  fun pluginMenu(plugins: List<PluginInfo>?, commands: List<SlashCommand>, names: List<String>, skills: List<String>?): ImmutableList<PluginEntry>
  //   plugins and skills null: no init yet
  fun withCommand(draft: String, command: String): String
  // chat/ConnectorMenu.kt (spec 17.5; deviation 24)
  data class McpBusyView(val name: String, val action: McpAction, val caption: String)   // ui/'s McpBusy, as chat/ sees it
  data class ConnectorRow(val key: String, val name: String, val label: String, val on: Boolean, val status: String?,
      val caption: String?, val error: String?, val switchable: Boolean)
  data class ConnectorMenuModel(val rows: ImmutableList<ConnectorRow>, val note: String?)
  const val NO_CONNECTORS: String = "No claude.ai connectors in this session."
  const val CONNECTORS_LAST_KNOWN: String = "Last known: the session is not running."
  fun connectorMenu(panel: McpPanelModel, busy: McpBusyView?, rowErrors: Map<String, String>, notice: String?): ConnectorMenuModel
  fun toggleAction(row: ConnectorRow): McpAction?   // null unless switchable; on → DISABLE, off → ENABLE
  // existing classes
  NoticeRow(+ usage: UsageCardModel? = null, markdown: Boolean = false, sourceUuid: String? = null)
  ComposerModel(+ plugins: ImmutableList<PluginEntry> = persistentListOf(), pluginsKnown: Boolean = false, skills: ImmutableList<String>? = null)
  //   set in the base's composerModel(state, known, knownCommands) (PR #9's signature; its models and commands fallbacks unchanged):
  //   skills = state.skills.takeIf { state.mcp.initCount > 0 }; plugins = pluginMenu(state.plugins.takeIf { state.mcp.initCount > 0 },
  //   <the model's own commands: the session's, else knownCommands'>, state.slashCommandNames, skills); pluginsKnown = initCount > 0 || plugins.isNotEmpty()
  ```
- Rules (spec 3.1–3.3):
  - `contextPopover`: rows for `tokens > 0` only; order `kind == "used"` by tokens descending, then `buffer`, then `free`, then deferred by tokens descending, then any other kind in the CLI's order, then a missing kind in the CLI's order; tone by `id` (`messages`, `mcp_tools`, `memory_files`, `system_prompt`, `free_space` → FREE, anything else NEUTRAL), never by `name`; `tokens` by `formatTokens`; `share` by `percentOf(tokens, maxTokens)`, `—` for a deferred row; `description` `"<name>, <tokens> tokens, <share>"`, or `"<name>, <tokens> tokens, deferred"`. Segments: the `used` rows in row order, `tokens / maxTokens`; none when `maxTokens` is null or 0. `value`: `"${formatTokens(total)} / ${formatWindow(max)} (${pct}%)"` with `pct = percentage ?: round(100.0 * total / max)` when both are known and `max > 0`, else `ContextFormat.value(ring)`, else null. Groups, in this order and only with items: `mcp_tools` `MCP tools` (label `"<server> · <tool>"` with `mcp__<server>__` removed from the name, the raw name when it lacks the prefix, the name alone without a server; `dim = loaded == false`), `mcp_instructions` `MCP server instructions` (label the server name), `memory_files` `Memory files` (label `displayPath`, tooltip `"<path> · <type>"` or the path alone), `skills` `Skills` (the name as sent), `agents` `Custom agents` (label the `agentType`, tooltip the `source` when set); `total` the sum of every item's tokens, `count` every item, `items` the first `GROUP_ITEM_CAP`, `more = count - items.size`.
  - `displayPath`: the project root first (path == root + separator + rest → rest), then the home (path == home → `~`; home + separator + rest → `~/rest`), a separator being `/` or `\`; anything else unchanged. String prefixes only, no file access.
  - `popoverStatus` (spec 3.1, Errors): `stopped` → `(null, BREAKDOWN_STOPPED, false, dimRows = hasRows)`; else `failedText != null` → `(null, failedText, error = true, dimRows = hasRows)` (the view puts `Retry` beside it; rows from an earlier answer stay below it, dimmed); else `loading` → `(BREAKDOWN_UPDATING, null, false, false)` with rows, `(null, BREAKDOWN_LOADING, false, false)` without; else `(null, null, false, false)`.
  - `percentOf`: `max <= 0` → `—`; else `String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.1f%%", 100.0 * tokens / max)`.
  - `usageCard`: facts from `session`: `Est. cost` `formatCost(totalCostUsd)`, `API` `formatElapsed(apiDurationMs)`, `Wall` `formatElapsed(wallDurationMs)`, each left out when absent (all three absent → `Shown([])`, and the view leaves the line out); `Loading` → `UsageSection.Loading`; `Failed`: `Rejected(m)` → `Error("Could not load usage: $m", true)`, `TimedOut(s)` → `Error("The CLI did not answer within $s s.", true)`, `Ended`/`NotRunning` → `Error("The session has ended.", false)`. `promptCache = promptCacheLine(noticeText)`. `models`: the session's active model first, then the CLI's order; label = `modelDisplayName(id, models)`, the base's (0.1.2) and the footer's own naming: the `displayName` of the first `ModelOption` other than the `default` entry whose `resolvedModel` or `value` equals the id, else of a family alias among the id's words (an entry with no `resolvedModel`; `parity-usage:1` has none), else the id; cost `formatCost(costUsd)`; rows `Input`, `Output`, `Cache read`, `Cache write` by `formatTokens` (absent → left out), then `Web searches` with the plain count when `webSearches > 0` (spec 16); `costShare` and `costShareText` from `costShare(costUsd, session.totalCostUsd)` (spec 16); an empty `model_usage` → `models` empty, and the view leaves `Breakdown` out. History: `Loading` → `Loading`; `Loaded(null)` → `Note("The CLI did not report what is using your limits.")`; `Loaded(h)` → `Shown(window(h.day, "Last 24 hours") to window(h.week, "Last 7 days"))`, where `window` is null for a window the CLI left out or one that has, after the label rule below, no behaviour line, no share and no tip (the view draws `WINDOW_EMPTY` for null); `Failed`: `Rejected(m)` → `Error("Could not read the usage history: $m", true)`, `TimedOut(s)` as above, `Ended`/`NotRunning` → `Error("The session has ended.", false)`. A window: caveat `"<label> · <n> sessions · <r> requests · approximate, overlapping measures · this machine only, excludes claude.ai"` (`<r>` is `formatTokens(requestCount)`, spec 16; each count part left out when absent); behaviours by `pct` descending (stable), label from `subagent_heavy` → `came from subagent-heavy sessions`, `long_context` → `came from sessions with a long context`, `large_tool_results` → `came from sessions with large tool results`, `max_effort` → `ran at max effort` (`known = true`); any other key takes the `title` of the window's first recommendation with the same key (`known = false`: the CLI's own words), and a key with neither is left out (probes 3.3: unknown keys are hidden); shares from `agents` (`Subagent`), `skills` (`Skill`), `plugins` (`Plugin`), `mcp_servers` (`MCP`) by `pct` descending (stable), `fraction = pct / 100f`, `pct` `"<n>%"`; `tips` every recommendation in the CLI's order (none → empty, and the view leaves the section out; spec 16). `cliText = noticeText`; `cliTextOpen` when the session part is `Failed` or `undrawnCliLines(noticeText)`: some non-blank line neither starts (after leading spaces) with `Total cost:`, `Total duration (API):`, `Total duration (wall):`, `Total code changes:`, `Usage by model:` or `Prompt cache (main):` nor is an indented line in the block `Usage by model:` opens (a model line, up to the next unindented line), so a subscriber's plan rows (docs) open the disclosure by themselves (spec 3.2).
  - `formatCost`: `usd <= 0` or NaN → `$0.00`; `< 0.005` → `<$0.01`; else `String.format(Locale.ROOT, "$%.2f", usd)`. `promptCacheLine`: the rest of the first line that starts with `Prompt cache (main):`, trimmed; null without one.
  - (spec 16) `costShare`: null when `costUsd` or `totalUsd` is null or NaN, or `totalUsd <= 0`; `share = (costUsd / totalUsd).coerceIn(0.0, 1.0)`; text `<1% of cost` when `0 < share < 0.005`, `>99% of cost` when `share >= 0.995`, else `"${round(100 * share)}% of cost"`. `tipsTitle`: `Tip` for 1, else `Tips`. `tipsToggle(count, open)`: null when `count <= TIPS_SHOWN`, else `Show ${count - TIPS_SHOWN} more` closed and `Show fewer` open.
  - `pluginMenu` (spec 3.3 with 17.3's rules): an entry's prefix is `name.substringBefore(':')` of a name with a `:` and a non-empty rest; a description's parenthesised prefix is the text between a leading `(` and the first `)` (none without both). `plugins` null (no init yet; spec 3.3: the init comes only with the first turn, the `initialize` answer at spawn) → one entry per distinct prefix, in the catalogue's order, that has at least one entry whose description has a parenthesised prefix, never `SYNCED_SKILLS_NAMESPACE`, version and source null; else the non-built-in plugins in the init's order, version and source the init's. A row belongs to plugin `p` when its prefix equals `p.name` exactly (a plugin with the same name twice gets the same rows twice); from the catalogue (`commands`) when it is not empty, else from `names` with description `""`. `key`: the name, `name#2`, `name#3`… for later entries with the same name. `label`: the parenthesised prefix of the plugin's own rows when every row that has one has the same one, else, and for a plugin with no such row, its `name`; verbatim. A row: `name` the part after the first `:`, `command` `"/<plugin>:<rest>"`, `description` the description without its parenthesised prefix and the space after it (`""` when that is all it had), `tooltip` that description, then `"\n" + argumentHint` when the hint is not blank, `kind` `SKILL` when `skills` is not null and lists the full name, or when `skills` is null and the entry's `aliases` hold its bare name, else `COMMAND`. `skills` and `commands` keep the catalogue's order. `withCommand`: `"$command " + draft.trimStart()`.
  - (spec 17) `connectorMenu`: the rows of `panel`'s `McpSource.CLAUDEAI` group only, in its order: `key` and `name` the row's, `label` its `displayName`, `on` `status != DISABLED`, except while `busy?.name == name` with `ENABLE` (true) or `DISABLE` (false); `status` the row's `label` unless the status is `CONNECTED` or `DISABLED` (then null); `caption` `busy.caption` on the busy row; `error` `rowErrors[name] ?: row.error`; `switchable` when `busy == null`, the panel is not `stopped` and the row's `actions` hold `ENABLE` or `DISABLE`. `note`: `notice`, else `CONNECTORS_LAST_KNOWN` when `panel.stopped` and there are rows, else `NO_CONNECTORS` when there are none, else null. `toggleAction`: null unless `switchable`; `DISABLE` when `on`, else `ENABLE`.
  - `ChatRowsBuilder.noticeRow`: `usage = entry.usage?.let { usageCard(it, entry.text, state.models, state.activeModel) }`, `markdown = entry.kind == LOCAL_COMMAND && entry.runCommand == "context"`, `sourceUuid = entry.sourceUuid`. Deviation 7: a `Frozen` turn keeps its usage notices (index and instance); `reopenReferencedTurns` reopens from the earliest frozen turn whose usage notice at that index is no longer the same instance.

**Files:**

- Modify: `.../ui/FooterControls.kt` (`ContextRing`, `RingControl`), `.../ui/TandemColors.kt`, `.../ui/PromptBar.kt` (builds the host), `.../ui/ChatScreen.kt` (the height), `.../ui/ChatPresenter.kt` (Markdown of a `markdown` notice), `.../ui/rows/NoticeItem.kt`, `.../ui/ChatPanel.kt` (passes `blocks`), `src/integrationTest/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/ToolWindowIdeTest.kt`
- Test: Create `src/test/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/ui/ContextBreakdownControllerTest.kt`; Modify `.../ui/ChatPresenterTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: D2a `ContextControls`, `ControlError`, `SessionManager.send`; D3 `contextPopover`, `ContextPopoverModel`, `ContextTone`.
- Produces (spec 4.4):
  ```kotlin
  internal sealed interface FetchState { data object Idle : FetchState; data object Loading : FetchState; data class Failed(val text: String) : FetchState; data object Stopped : FetchState }
  internal class ContextBreakdownController(id: SessionId, breakdown: StateFlow<ContextBreakdown?>, ring: StateFlow<ContextUsage?>, turnsEnded: StateFlow<Int>,
      status: StateFlow<SessionStatus>, controls: ContextControls, scope: CoroutineScope, home: String?, projectRoot: String?,
      dispatcher: CoroutineDispatcher = Dispatchers.Default) {
      val model: StateFlow<ContextPopoverModel>; val fetch: StateFlow<FetchState>; val rowsOpen: StateFlow<Boolean>; fun toggleRows(); fun retry(); fun close() }
  internal class ContextPopoverHost(val open: (CoroutineScope) -> ContextBreakdownController, val busy: StateFlow<Boolean>, val maxHeight: Dp, val onDetailed: () -> Unit, val onUsage: () -> Unit)   // onUsage: spec 16
  @Composable internal fun ContextRing(context: StateFlow<ContextUsage?>, popover: ContextPopoverHost? = null)
  internal object TandemColors { fun contextTone(tone: ContextTone, dark: Boolean): Color? }   // null for NEUTRAL and FREE (theme colours)
  @Composable internal fun NoticeItem(row: NoticeRow, blocks: ImmutableList<MarkdownBlock>? = null, onOpenMcp: () -> Unit = {})
  ```
- Controller rules (spec 3.1): every request on `dispatcher`, under a child `Job` of `scope` that `close()` cancels. `model = combine(breakdown, ring) { b, r -> contextPopover(b, r, home, projectRoot) }` built on `dispatcher`. Requests: one at construction unless `status` is `STOPPED`; one on every change of `turnsEnded` after its first value; one when `status` leaves `STOPPED`; `retry()`. While one runs, a new trigger runs once after it (at most one waiting). Answers: `Ok` → `Idle` (the reducer already holds the breakdown); `Failed(Rejected(m))` → `Failed("Could not load the breakdown: $m")`; `Failed(TimedOut(s))` → `Failed("The CLI did not answer within $s s.")`; `NotRunning`/`Ended` → `Stopped`; `status == STOPPED` → `Stopped`, no request. While a request runs, `fetch` is `Loading`. `rowsOpen` starts `false` (the popover opens compact, spec 3.1) and `toggleRows()` flips it.
- Popover (spec 3.1, 3.5, 3.7), the `PopupMenu` of `RingControl`'s `if (open)` branch, `Modifier.testTag("context-popover").semantics { contentDescription = "Context window breakdown" }`, width `min(360 dp, chat column − 32 dp)` (at least 280 dp), height capped at `popover.maxHeight`; its status is `popoverStatus(fetch is Loading, (fetch as? Failed)?.text, fetch is Stopped, model.rows.isNotEmpty())` (D3), never decided in the composable: the header row (`Context window` dim, then `model.value` plus `headerSuffix`, then `›` / `⌄`) is a `selectableItem` that toggles `rowsOpen` and keeps the popover open (the hold-open flag below), `contentDescription = "Context window categories"`, `stateDescription` `Expanded` / `Collapsed`; under it `line` when set, in `text.error` with a `Retry` item when `error`; the bar; the header row, the status line, the bar and `See detailed breakdown` show in both states, and only while `rowsOpen` the category rows (dimmed when `dimRows`) and the groups below; the bar (`Canvas`, 4 dp, rounded, track `dim` at 30 %, segments in `contextTone` or `dim`, each non-zero one at least 1 px); passive category rows (`semantics(mergeDescendants = true) { contentDescription = row.description }`, swatch, name, tokens, share); groups collapsed on open, each a `selectableItem` whose click toggles it and keeps the popover open (a hold-open flag read by `onDismissRequest`, as `FooterControl`'s `turning`), `›` / `⌄`, title, total, count, `contentDescription = "<title>, <count> items, <total> tokens"`, `stateDescription` `Expanded` / `Collapsed`; expanded items passive, dim when `item.dim`, tooltip when set, then `and N more` when `more > 0`; then a separator and `See detailed breakdown` (`See detailed breakdown (after this turn)`, disabled, while `busy`), which closes the popover and calls `onDetailed`; under it (spec 16.2) `Show session usage` (`Show session usage (after this turn)`, disabled, while `busy`), which closes the popover and calls `onUsage`. The ring's `stateDescription` is the header value. If Jewel's menu does not honour the height cap with scrolling (spec 11 item 6), the category rows and groups go into one passive item holding a `VerticallyScrollableContainer`, `Retry`, `See detailed breakdown` and `Show session usage` stay selectable items outside it; say which in the commit body.
- Wiring: `PromptBar` passes `ContextPopoverHost(open = { scope -> ContextBreakdownController(handle.id, handle.state.map { it.contextBreakdown }…, presenter.context, handle.state.map { it.turnsEnded }…, handle.status, manager, scope, System.getProperty("user.home"), project.basePath) }, busy = presenter.busy, maxHeight = 60 % of the tool window, onDetailed = { manager.send(handle.id, "/context") }, onUsage = { manager.send(handle.id, "/usage") })` (the draft and the attachments untouched); `ChatScreen` measures the tool window's height and passes it to `PromptBar`. `ChatPresenter.renderRow`: a `NoticeRow` with `markdown` gets `RenderedRow(row, blocks(row.key, row.text))` (parsed off the EDT, cached by key and text), every other notice `blocks = null`; `NoticeItem` draws `Markdown(blocks, …)` as `TextRowItem` does when `blocks != null`, keeping the `Reply to /context` semantics.
- IDE harness: none new.

### Task D5: ui-usage-card

**Depends on:** D4 (Opus; reviewer Opus). Carries IDE test 2.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/main/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/ui/rows/UsageCardView.kt`
- Modify: `.../ui/rows/NoticeItem.kt`, `.../ui/ChatPanel.kt` (and the caller that wires `onOpenMcp` today, which gains `onUsageRetry`), `src/integrationTest/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/ToolWindowIdeTest.kt`
- Test: the IDE test only (the card's model is D3's, its requests D2a's).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: D3 `UsageCardModel`, `UsageSection`, `USAGE_LOADING`, `HISTORY_LOADING`, `WINDOW_EMPTY`, `NoticeRow.usage/sourceUuid`; D2a `SessionManager.retryUsage`, `UsagePart`.
- Produces:
  ```kotlin
  @Composable internal fun UsageCardView(model: UsageCardModel, key: String, onRetry: (UsagePart) -> Unit)
  @Composable internal fun NoticeItem(row: NoticeRow, blocks: ImmutableList<MarkdownBlock>? = null, onOpenMcp: () -> Unit = {}, onUsageRetry: (uuid: String, part: UsagePart) -> Unit = { _, _ -> })
  ```
- Rules (spec 3.2, 3.5, 3.7): `NoticeItem` draws `UsageCardView(row.usage, row.key) { part -> onUsageRetry(row.sourceUuid!!, part) }` in place of the text when `row.usage != null && row.sourceUuid != null`; the wiring calls `manager.retryUsage(handle.id, uuid, part)`. Such a row is not merged: today `NoticeItem` merges every LOCAL_COMMAND row into one node (`semantics(mergeDescendants = true) { contentDescription = "Reply to /…" }`); with `row.usage != null` its `Reply to /<command>` description sits on a container that does not merge (`semantics { … }` without `mergeDescendants`), so the card, the window control, each `Retry` and the disclosure stay their own nodes; every other notice keeps today's merge. The card: a bordered box in the chat column (6 dp radius, 12 dp padding, the polish cards' frame), `testTag("usage-card")`, `contentDescription = "Usage for this session"` (not merged), text in a `SelectionContainer`, every section stacked and wrapping. `This session`: the facts as one wrapping line of `label value` pairs (left out when the list is empty), then `Prompt cache` and `promptCache` when set; `Loading` → `USAGE_LOADING`; `Error` → its text in `text.error` with `Retry` (→ `onRetry(SESSION)`) when `retry`. `Breakdown` (left out when `models` is empty): per `ModelBlock` its label and cost, then (spec 16.2) a 4 dp bar of `costShare` with `costShareText` when set, then the rows (`Web searches` among them when present). `What's using your limits?`: `SegmentedControl` `24h` | `7d` (`contentDescription = "Usage window"`, the choice `remember(key)`, `24h` first), the window's caveat, behaviour lines (`<pct>` then the label, plain text), share rows (name, kind dim, a 4 dp bar of `fraction`, `pct`), (spec 16.2) `tipsTitle(tips.size)` and the first `TIPS_SHOWN` tips (every one while open), each its title and, under it in the dim tier, its detail when present, plain text, then `tipsToggle` as a disclosure row (`remember(key)`, `stateDescription` `Expanded` / `Collapsed`), the section left out when `tips` is empty; a null window (the model marks an absent or empty one, D3) → `WINDOW_EMPTY`; `Loading` → `HISTORY_LOADING`; `Error` with `Retry` (→ `onRetry(HISTORY)`); `Note` → its text. `CLI output`: a disclosure row (`›` / `⌄`, `stateDescription` `Expanded` / `Collapsed`, open initially when `cliTextOpen`, `remember(key)`) showing `cliText` plain in the editor style. Nothing in the card takes the focus by itself.
- IDE harness: `waitForTextContaining(text)` beside `waitForText` (which matches `@visible_text` whole): `contains(@visible_text, '$text')`, for a part of a wrapping line.

- Rules (spec 12.1 to 12.5):
  - `composerKey`'s Enter branch: `shift || alt` → null; `paletteOpen && ctrlOrMeta` → null; `cardOpen` → `QueueLater` when `ctrlOrMeta && midTurn` under D8's `queue-during-card` outcome **waits**, else `BusyRefused` (spec 12.2's card notice); `!busy || shellOnly` → `Send`; `!midTurn` → `BusyRefused`; `ctrlOrMeta` → `QueueLater`; else `Send`. `busy` stays PromptBar's (`composer.busy || composer.shellRunning`, which Esc's Stop also reads) and `shellOnly = !composer.busy && composer.shellRunning`: a running `!` alone never refuses Enter, and D10 holds the prompt behind its escape (spec 12.4 rule 2), capabilities or not. `composerFallback` keeps `Swallow` for a plain Enter with a card open, no longer reached from the Enter branch; a busy Enter no longer reaches it. Ctrl and Meta are both accepted on every OS.
  - `PromptBar`: `Send` → `sendDraft(ui, sender, later = sendsLater(isSlashCommand(text, composer), attachments.size, composer.busy, queueKey = false))`; `QueueLater` → `sendDraft(ui, sender, later = true)`; `BusyRefused` → `ui.composerNotice.value = busyRefusal(composer, cardOpen = <a card is open>)` (with an empty draft a card's refusal stays silent); a `!` draft while PromptBar's `busy` (`composer.busy || composer.shellRunning`) → `busyRefusal(composer, shell = true)`, nothing runs and the draft is kept (MCP 12.6's silent swallow, now said; today `manager.runShell` ignores a second command and the draft is cleared); a busy draft that `isSlashCommand` and is not intercepted follows the slash-command rule below (a draft that merely starts with `/`, such as a path, is a prompt); a busy draft with attachments follows the image rule below; the four intercepted commands act at once, busy or not, as today. The placeholder is `composerPlaceholder(composer, SystemInfo.isMac)`. While busy with `midTurnSends` and `canSend`, an icon button `Send` (`contentDescription = "Send"`, tooltip `sendNowTooltip(SystemInfo.isMac)`) sits left of Stop, a click acting as Enter. A palette pick passes `midTurn = composer.midTurnSends`; its `Send(text, later)` goes out through the sender with `later`. A `LaunchedEffect(handle.id)` collects `composer.returned`: when not empty, `restoreReturned(ui, returned, attach)`, then `manager.takeReturned(handle.id, returned.map { it.uuid })`. The context popover's `busy` and the reply row's `busy` (both the presenter's `busy`, which follows `ComposerModel.busy`) now count waiting messages too (spec 13.1, 16.2).
  - Slash commands while busy follow D8's `queue-later-command` outcome (spec 12.1). **After the turn**: `sendsLater` as written (a slash command goes `later`), `pick` as below, and the ChatRowsBuilderTest `a later /context runs as its own local turn after the running one`. **Otherwise**, or when D8 could not record it: `sendsLater` drops its `command` clause; a busy Enter or Ctrl/⌘+Enter on a draft that `isSlashCommand` and is not intercepted → `busyRefusal(composer, command = true)`, the draft kept; a busy palette pick stays `Ignored` and shows the same notice. D10 needs no change either way: its rules key on the lifecycle lines.
  - Images while busy follow D8's image outcome for `queue-later-command` (spec 12.1). **Runs**: `sendsLater` as written (a draft with images goes `later`, whichever key). **Otherwise**, or when D8 could not record it: `sendsLater` drops its `imageCount` clause; a busy Enter or Ctrl/⌘+Enter on a draft with attachments → `busyRefusal(composer, images = true)`, the draft and its attachments kept (no frame, so nothing depends on a request the CLI was not seen to answer). D10 needs no change either way.
  - `restoreReturned`: the returned texts in order, joined by a blank line, ahead of the current draft (a blank line between them and the draft; none when the draft is empty), the cursor at the end; each image's base64 decoded on `Dispatchers.Default`, then attached once through `attach` on the caller's context (the EDT; hard rule 1).
  - `ChatRowsBuilder`: after the working row, one `QueuedRow` per `pendingSends` entry, in order: key `q:<uuid>`, images as `UserRow` makes them, `caption = queuedCaption(send)`, `withdrawable = send.later` (every send when D8's `queue-cancel-steer` outcome is **withdrawable**), `withdrawing = stage == WITHDRAWING`. A folded `User` is a `UserRow` with `caption = FOLDED_CAPTION` and starts no turn: a turn splits only at a `User` that is not folded, so one CLI turn is one visual turn with one footer. `withUniqueKey` and row reuse cover `QueuedRow`; once started, the message's row is the `UserRow` with the user row's own key.
  - `queuedCaption`: `WITHDRAWING` → `Withdrawing…`; `HELD` → `Waits for the shell command's output to go out`; `QUEUED` and `later` → `Queued · sends when Claude is done`; `QUEUED` and not `later` → `Sent · Claude reads it at its next step`.
  - `composerPlaceholder`: busy with `midTurnSends` → `↵ to send, ⌘↵ to queue for later` on macOS, `Enter to send, Ctrl+Enter to queue for later` elsewhere; busy without → `Claude is working · Esc to stop`; idle → `Type / for commands`.
  - `busyRefusal`: `shell` → `Shell commands run when Claude is done: wait for the reply, or press Esc to stop.` while `model.busy || model.shellRunning`, else null; `cardOpen` → `Answer the card above first, then send.`; not busy → null; `command` → `Slash commands run when Claude is done: wait for the reply, or press Esc to stop.` (the **otherwise** outcome only); `images` → `Images go out when Claude is done: wait for the reply, or press Esc to stop.` (the image's **otherwise** outcome only); `!capabilitiesKnown` → `Claude is still starting: send again in a moment.`; `!midTurnSends` → `This claude CLI does not take messages while it works: wait for the reply, or press Esc to stop.`; else null.
  - `sendNowTooltip`: `Send now · ⌘↵ queues it for after this turn` on macOS, `Send now · Ctrl+Enter queues it for after this turn` elsewhere.
  - `pick`: with D8's **after the turn** outcome, busy with `midTurn` → what an idle pick sends, with `later = true` (spec 12.1); busy without → `Ignored`, as today; intercepted rows and Tab's insert unchanged.
  - `QueuedRowItem`: the user bubble's frame at the dim tier with its text and thumbnails, the caption under it in the footer size; when `withdrawable`, two 16 dp icon buttons `Edit queued message` (→ `onWithdraw(true)`) and `Cancel queued message` (→ `onWithdraw(false)`), disabled while `withdrawing`; `testTag("queued-row")`; the text and caption one merged node, each button its own; nothing takes the focus by itself. `ChatPanel` wires `onWithdraw = { manager.withdraw(handle.id, uuid, it) }`; `UserBubble` draws `caption` under the bubble in the dim tier when set.
- IDE harness: none new (`waitForTextContaining` is D5's).

### Task D12b: chat-ui-fork-at

**Depends on:** D12a, D11 (`ChatRowsBuilder`, `ChatPresenter`), D8's committed `fork-at-live` and `fork-at-busy` outcomes (Opus; reviewer Opus). Carries spec 9.7's IDE test 3 (ide). Spec 13.1 (States and tooltip), 13.2 (the chat and ui part). With D8's `fork-at-live` outcome **refused or unlike**, `SessionManager.fork(id, atMessage)` (D12a's) first stops the original's live idle process with `handle.stopIfIdle()` and forks only once no process runs; a refused stop (a turn started between the click and the stop) forks nothing, logs it and gives `ForkResult.Refused.BUSY`, so the chat says `Wait for the reply to finish`, and the button then reads `WAIT`; `SessionManagerTest` gains `a fork at a reply stops the original's idle process first` (open the seeded original, which spawns; `fork(seeded, "<uuid-14>")` → the original's status `STOPPED` before the fork's `__spawn__`; with a turn held open by the fake's delay knobs, `forkResult(seeded, "<uuid-14>")` == `Refused.BUSY` and no new `__spawn__`), and IDE test 3 runs as written, since its original, opened from the sidebar, has a live idle process that the click now stops first. (A `WAIT` whenever the original has a live process would disable the button on every reply of the session in use, spec 13.1.) With D8's `fork-at-busy` outcome **matches**, `forkState` drops its `busy` parameter (`forkAt` null → `UNAVAILABLE`, else `ENABLED`; only ended turns' replies carry actions), `ReplyHost` loses `busy`, and the tests below that expect `WAIT` expect `ENABLED`. Everything else stands. Whichever branches D8 settled, D12b writes their content tests below (`FixtureDecodeTest` `fork-at-live - …`, `fork-at-busy - …`; Review Focus 12).

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/main/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/chat/ChatRow.kt` (`MessageActions.forkAt`, `ForkState`, `forkState`), `.../chat/ChatRowsBuilder.kt` (`forkAt` per reply; `ChatRows.latestReply` and `latestReply()` removed), `.../ui/ChatPresenter.kt` (`latestReply` removed, `RenderedChat`), `.../ui/rows/MessageActionsRow.kt` (`ReplyHost`, the tooltip), `.../ui/ChatScreen.kt`, `.../ui/SessionActions.kt`, `src/integrationTest/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/ToolWindowIdeTest.kt`
- Test: `src/test/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/protocol/FixtureDecodeTest.kt` (D8's two fork recordings), `src/test/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/chat/ChatRowsBuilderTest.kt` (its `latestReply` tests replaced), `.../ui/ChatPresenterTest.kt` (its `latestReply` test replaced), `.../ui/SessionActionsTest.kt`, and in the **refused or unlike** branch `.../session/SessionManagerTest.kt` (with `.../session/SessionManager.kt` modified)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: D12a `ChatEntry.Assistant.lastUuid`, `SessionManager.fork(id, atMessage)`; D11's `busy`.
- Produces (spec 13.2):
  ```kotlin
  data class MessageActions(val entryId: String, val markdown: String, val atMillis: Long?, val forkAt: String? = null)
  enum class ForkState { ENABLED, WAIT, UNAVAILABLE }                   // NOT_LATEST removed
  fun forkState(forkAt: String?, busy: Boolean): ForkState          // forkAt null → UNAVAILABLE; busy → WAIT; else ENABLED
  data class ChatRows(val rows: ImmutableList<ChatRow>, val card: PendingCard?, val moreWaiting: Int)   // latestReply removed
  internal class ReplyHost(val onCopy: (String) -> Unit, val onFork: (forkAt: String) -> Unit, val busy: State<Boolean>)
  fun fork(id: SessionId, at: String? = null)                        // SessionActions: manager.forkResult(id, at); Refused.NO_TRANSCRIPT → the notice "This session has no transcript to fork yet", NOT_IN_HISTORY → "This reply cannot be forked: the CLI sent no id for it", BUSY → "Wait for the reply to finish" (hard rule 5: the real reason)
  ```
- Rules: `ChatRowsBuilder` gives each ended turn's reply `forkAt` = the `lastUuid` of the turn's last main-thread `Assistant` that has one (scanning the turn backwards), null when none has; the point lives on the row, so a frozen turn keeps it; a turn whose reply is only a notice has no reply row and no button. `MessageActionsRow`: `forkState(actions.forkAt, reply.busy.value)` in a `derivedStateOf`; a click calls `onFork(forkAt)` only when `ENABLED`; tooltips: `ENABLED` `Fork from here` with the dim line `Starts a new session from this point. This one stays as it is.`, `WAIT` `Wait for the reply to finish`, `UNAVAILABLE` `This reply cannot be forked: the CLI sent no id for it`; the accessible name stays `Fork from here`. `ChatScreen`: `ReplyHost(::copyToClipboard, { at -> actions.fork(handle.id, at) }, busy)`. The sidebar's `Fork` still forks the whole session (`fork(id)`). `NOT_LATEST`, `Only the latest reply can be forked`, `ChatRows.latestReply`, `ChatRowsBuilder.latestReply()` and `ChatPresenter.latestReply` are gone: `grep -rn 'NOT_LATEST\|Only the latest reply\|latestReply' src/main` → no hit.

**Depends on:** D13a, D12b (`ComposerKeys`, `PromptBar`), D6a (`FooterControls`) (Opus; reviewer Opus). Carries spec 9.7's IDE test 4 (ide, deviation 17) and rewrites D4's and D6a's keyboard IDE tests, which reach the ring and `+` with Shift+Tab from the text area until this task takes that key (spec 9.5). Spec 14.1, 14.2, 14.3 (the chat and ui part); `ui-accessibility`.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/main/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/ui/CyclePermissionModeAction.kt`

- Test: Create `src/test/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/ui/CyclePermissionModeActionTest.kt`; Modify `.../chat/ModesTest.kt`, `.../ui/ComposerKeysTest.kt`, `.../ui/FooterControlsTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: D13a `SessionManager.cyclePermissionMode`, `ChatState.rejectedPermissionModes`.
- Produces (spec 14.3):
  ```kotlin
  // chat/Modes.kt, members of object Modes
  val CYCLE: List<PermissionMode>   // [DEFAULT, ACCEPT_EDITS, PLAN, AUTO], the CLI's order
  fun next(current: PermissionMode?, autoSupported: Boolean, rejected: Set<PermissionMode> = emptySet()): PermissionMode
  fun cycleFrom(state: ChatState): PermissionMode   // next(state.permissionMode, shownModel(…)?.supportsAutoMode == true, state.rejectedPermissionModes)
  // ui/CyclePermissionModeAction.kt
  const val CYCLE_MODE_ACTION_ID: String = "Tandem.CyclePermissionMode"
  fun interface ComposerModes { fun cycle(): PermissionMode? }
  val COMPOSER_MODES: DataKey<ComposerModes>          // DataKey.create("dev.daarkolabs.tandem.composerModes")
  class CyclePermissionModeAction : DumbAwareAction()  // getActionUpdateThread() = BGT; update: isEnabledAndVisible = e.getData(COMPOSER_MODES) != null;
                                                       // actionPerformed: cycle(), then AccessibleAnnouncerUtil.announce "Permission mode <Modes.label(mode)>"; logs the wire name only
  // ui/ComposerKeys.kt
  ComposerAction.FocusNext                             // unmodified Key.Tab, after the palette rules; Shift+Tab never handled here
  // ui/FooterControls.kt
  internal fun modeMenuHint(shortcut: String): String?   // "$shortcut to cycle"; null when blank
  // ui/ChatUiState.kt
  val openMenus: MutableIntState   // how many of the mode, model and effort menus, the ring's popover and the + menu are open
  // ui/CyclePermissionModeAction.kt
  internal fun composerModesFor(modes: ComposerModes, openMenus: Int): ComposerModes?   // modes while openMenus == 0, else null
  ```
  `plugin.xml`: `<actions><action id="Tandem.CyclePermissionMode" class="dev.daarkolabs.tandem.ui.CyclePermissionModeAction" text="Cycle Permission Mode" description="Switch the Tandem chat to the next permission mode: Manual, Accept edits, Plan, then Auto when the model supports it"><keyboard-shortcut keymap="$default" first-keystroke="shift TAB"/></action></actions>` (the plugin's first action; no "Claude Code" in it, hard rule 8).
- Rules (spec 14.1, 14.2): `next` walks `CYCLE` from the current mode's slot, wrapping, and returns the first allowed mode (`AUTO` only with `autoSupported`; never one in `rejected`); start slots: a mode in `CYCLE` its own, `BYPASS_PERMISSIONS` Plan's, `DONT_ASK` Auto's, null Manual's (the first press asks for Accept edits); fallback `DEFAULT`; it never returns bypass or dontAsk. `PromptBar`: `val modes = remember(manager, workspace) { ComposerModes { manager.cyclePermissionMode(currentHandle.id, Modes::cycleFrom)?.also { workspace.state.lastPermissionMode = it.wire } } }` (`currentHandle` through `rememberUpdatedState`); the text area's `provideData` sets `COMPOSER_MODES` to `composerModesFor(modes, ui.openMenus.intValue)` beside `PASTE_PROVIDER`, leaving the key out when that is null, so only while no footer menu, the ring's popover or the `+` menu is open (spec 7): `FooterControl` (mode, model, effort), `RingControl` (its popover) and the `+` menu's holder each count themselves in with `DisposableEffect(open) { if (open) ui.openMenus.intValue++; onDispose { if (open) ui.openMenus.intValue-- } }`, reaching `ChatUiState` as `menuRequest` does (passed down where it is not yet); `FocusNext` → `LocalFocusManager.current.moveFocus(FocusDirection.Next)` (an open palette's Tab still inserts; typed tabs end, pasted ones land). The mode menu's header row: `Mode`, a weight spacer, then `modeMenuHint(KeymapUtil.getFirstKeyboardShortcutText(CYCLE_MODE_ACTION_ID))` in the dim tier, left out when null. Esc with nothing to close stays unconsumed, as today (checked in `runIde`).

### Task D14: shimmer

**Depends on:** nothing in this round but the base's `Reduce motion` (`TandemSettings.State.reduceMotion`, `LocalReducedMotion`) and 0.1.2's orbs (`OrbIndicator`, `Spark`) (Opus; reviewer Opus). It shares `ide/TandemSessions.kt` with D6b, `ui/rows/` with D5 and D11 and `ToolWindowIdeTest.kt` with the IDE-test tasks, so it runs only between two chain commits, on a clean tree, never beside a Kotlin task: a chain step that can move to any point where the chain waits for a recording. A drawn-motion test, required and timeboxed (deviation 19): `MotionPixelsTest`, else IDE test 5 (ide), else the plain statement that only the shimmer's frame hook and the orbs' frames (`OrbIndicatorTest`) are pinned. Spec 15. The orbs are 0.1.2's as shipped, which the owner approved: this task changes nothing in `chat/OrbIndicator.kt`, `ui/rows/Spark.kt` or `OrbIndicatorTest.kt`, neither their choreography, colour, size or frame rate nor `Spark`'s parameters.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/main/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/ui/rows/Shimmer.kt`
- Modify: `.../chat/WorkingIndicator.kt`, `.../ui/rows/WorkingRowItem.kt`, `.../ui/rows/ToolRowItem.kt` (`ToolLine`), `.../ui/rows/ThinkingRowItem.kt`, `.../ide/TandemSettingsConfigurable.kt` (the comment), `.../ide/TandemSettings.kt` and `.../ide/TandemSessions.kt` (KDoc only), `src/integrationTest/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/ToolWindowIdeTest.kt` (a `working-row` screenshot; IDE test 5 only under deviation 19)
- Test: `src/test/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/chat/WorkingIndicatorTest.kt`, `.../ui/rows/WorkingRowItemTest.kt` (the base's composed test, extended); Create `src/test/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/ui/rows/MotionLoopsTest.kt` and `src/test/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/ui/rows/MotionPixelsTest.kt`
- Unchanged (0.1.2's): `.../chat/OrbIndicator.kt`, `.../ui/rows/Spark.kt`, `src/test/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/chat/OrbIndicatorTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (the base, 0.1.2): `Spark(animated: Boolean, tint: Color)`, which draws `OrbIndicator.frame(elapsed, animated)` on a square `lineHeight(smallTextStyle())` high, with its own loop (`withFrameMillis`, then `delay(33)`) and `clearAndSetSemantics {}`; `OrbIndicator.STILL`; `WorkingRowItem`'s `Spark(view.animated, tint)` call, kept as it is.
- Produces (spec 15.3):
  ```kotlin
  // chat/WorkingIndicator.kt, members of object WorkingIndicator
  const val SHIMMER_START_MS: Long = 600L; const val SHIMMER_SWEEP_MS: Long = 900L; const val SHIMMER_PERIOD_MS: Long = 3_000L
  const val SHIMMER_FRAME_MS: Long = 33L   // the sweep's pause between frames: at most about 30 frames a second (spec 15.2)
  fun shimmerProgress(elapsedMs: Long): Float?   // null before 600 ms and while resting; else (1 − cos(π · s / 900)) / 2 for s = (t − 600) mod 3000 < 900
  fun msToNextSweep(elapsedMs: Long): Long       // at least 1
  data class View(val mode: Mode, val animated: Boolean, val text: String, val accessibleLabel: String, val shimmerEnd: Int = 0)
  //   shimmerEnd: "${row.step}…".length while RUNNING and not under reduced motion; else 0
  // ui/rows/Shimmer.kt
  @Composable internal fun rememberShimmer(active: Boolean, pause: suspend (Long) -> Unit = { delay(it) }): State<Float?>
  internal suspend fun runShimmer(active: Boolean, set: (Float?) -> Unit, pause: suspend (Long) -> Unit = { delay(it) })   // its loop
  internal fun Modifier.shimmer(active: Boolean, progress: () -> Float?, endX: () -> Float, highlight: Color): Modifier
  // ui/rows/ToolRowItem.kt
  ToolLine(…, shimmer: Boolean = false, onClick)   // a new parameter before the trailing onClick
  // ui/rows/ThinkingRowItem.kt
  @Composable internal fun thinkingShimmer(row: ThinkingRow): Boolean   // row.streaming && !reducedMotion()
  // ui/rows/WorkingRowItem.kt: workingView, LocalReducedMotion, reducedMotion() and the Spark(view.animated, tint) call stay as the base has them
  //   (their code; the KDocs of workingView, LocalReducedMotion and reducedMotion() may read: the orbs stand still and nothing shimmers)
  ```
- Rules (spec 15.1, 15.2): `rememberShimmer(active)` runs `runShimmer(active, { value = it }, pause)` in a `LaunchedEffect(active)`: null when not active; else `pause(msToNextSweep(…))` until a sweep, then, while it lasts, `withFrameMillis { set(shimmerProgress(it - start)) }` and `pause(SHIMMER_FRAME_MS)` in turn (`start` the loop's first frame time; at most about 30 frames a second, as the orbs' loop, spec 15.2) until `shimmerProgress` is null again. `Modifier.shimmer`: only while `active`, a `graphicsLayer(compositingStrategy = Offscreen)` and `drawWithContent { drawContent(); progress() ?: return; drawRect(Brush.horizontalGradient(Transparent, highlight, Transparent) over a band of half-width 32 dp whose centre moves from −32 dp to end + 32 dp, size = (end, height), blendMode = SrcAtop) }`, `end` being `endX()` clamped to the layer's width, for the band's path and its size alike. `WorkingRowItem`: one `Text` with one string, `onTextLayout = { stepEnd = it.getHorizontalPosition(view.shimmerEnd, usePrimaryDirection = true) }`, `val shimmer = rememberShimmer(view.shimmerEnd > 0)` and `Modifier.shimmer(view.shimmerEnd > 0, { shimmer.value }, { stepEnd }, JewelTheme.globalColors.text.normal)`, `view` being the base's `workingView(…)`; the base stays `text.info`; the orbs' `Spark` before the text, the row's merged semantics (`Claude is working`) and `testTag("working-row")` unchanged. `ThinkingRowItem` passes `shimmer = thinkingShimmer(row)` (the band crosses its whole `Thinking… · N tokens` label); running tool rows pass nothing. `ToolLine` calls `val s = rememberShimmer(shimmer)` and puts `Modifier.shimmer(shimmer, { s.value }, { Float.POSITIVE_INFINITY }, JewelTheme.globalColors.text.normal)` on its label `Text` only, never on the `Row`, whose stats and chevron must not shimmer; `endX` is clamped to that `Text`'s width, so the band crosses exactly the label. The settings comment reads `The orbs and the shimmer beside Claude's working line stand still.` The KDocs that say only the spark stands still (`TandemSettings.State.reduceMotion`, `TandemSessions.reduceMotion`, `WorkingRowItem`'s `workingView`, `LocalReducedMotion` and `reducedMotion()`) say instead that the orbs stand still and nothing shimmers; their code stays.

- Rules (spec 17.2, 17.4, 3.5, 3.7, 7):
  - `Connectors` on page 1 now `show(PLUS_CONNECTORS)`. While the menu is open on that page, `PromptBar` holds one `McpPanelController` for its handle, built as `McpSheet` builds its own (`handle.mcpServers`, `handle.mcpAuth`, `handle.mcpLoginHinted`, `handle.status`, `manager`, `sessions.browserOpener`, the composition's scope, `endReason = { endNotice(handle.state.value) }`), `remember`ed on the handle and the page, and `close()`d in a `DisposableEffect` when the page leaves or the menu closes; so a page opened on a session without a process starts it within the cap (`startForMcp`), as the panel does. The page's model is `connectorMenu(rows, busy?.let { McpBusyView(it.name, it.action, it.caption) }, rowErrors, connectorPageNotice(rows, start, message))` from the controller's flows. The menu and the MCP panel are never open together: `Manage connectors` closes the menu before it opens the panel.
  - `connectorsPage`: `‹ Connectors` (→ `onBack`: `show(PLUS_MAIN)`), a separator, `Browse connectors` (`General.Web`), `Manage connectors` (`General.Settings`), a separator, one `selectableItem` per row: icon `Webreferences.Server`; the `label`, one line, ellipsised, the full `name` in its tooltip; under it, dim, `caption ?: status` when either is set; under that, in `text.error`, one line of `error` when set, the whole text in its tooltip; at the end `ConnectorSwitch(row.on, row.switchable)`; `enabled = row.switchable`; `semantics(mergeDescendants = true) { contentDescription = "<label> connector" plus the second and third lines when shown; role = Role.Switch; stateDescription = if (on) "On" else "Off" }`. Then the `note` when set, passive, dim (in `text.error` when it is the controller's `message`). A row's click or Enter: `hold()`, then `toggleAction(row)?.let { controller.run(it, row.name) }`: the controller's own request path under the handle's `mcpActions` lock and its follow-up `mcp_status`, nothing else. Names are config text: plain `Text`.
  - `Browse connectors`: the menu closes, then `scope.launch { sessions.browserOpener.open(CONNECTORS_URL) }` (the opener goes to IO itself; `tandem.browserOpener=log` writes `__browse__` to the fake's log). `Manage connectors`: the menu closes, then `ui.mcpPanelOpen.value = true`. Logs: the page's opening and each toggle's outcome by request id; never a connector's name or the address.
  - The shortcut (spec 17.4): `PromptBar` sets `ui.attach = ComposerAttach { ComposerImages.chooseImagesLater(project, onChosen = attachFiles) }` (the `+` row's own path, an EDT event of its own) in a `DisposableEffect(handle.id)` and clears it on dispose; `ChatScreen`'s root gets `Modifier.provideData { composerAttachFor(ui.attach, ui.openMenus.intValue)?.let { set(COMPOSER_ATTACH, it) } }`, so the key is there while the focus is anywhere in the selected session's chat pane and no footer menu, popover or `+` menu is open, and never in the editor or the sidebar. `Add files or photos`' `selectableItem` passes `keybinding = addFilesKeybinding()`, read when the menu opens, so a rebinding shows at the next opening and an unbound action shows nothing.
  - `ConnectorSwitch`: Global Constraints' exact values (spec 17.2); the knob at the track's end for `on`, its start for off; nothing animates.

  ```kotlin
      val tabs: StateFlow<List<SessionId>>; fun close()
  }
  ```

  `plugin.xml`, inside `<actions>`: `<action id="Tandem.NewSession" class="dev.daarkolabs.tandem.ui.NewSessionAction" text="New Session" description="Start a new Tandem session with the last model, effort and permission mode"/>`, no `<keyboard-shortcut>`.
- Rules (spec 18.1 to 18.4):
  - `sessionTabs`: keeps the entries of `open` with `status != STOPPED || id == selected || attention`, in `open`'s order (so a tab that leaves and comes back takes its old place); `label` `NEW_SESSION_LABEL` when `title == id`, else `title` cut to `TAB_LABEL_MAX` characters plus `…` when longer; `tooltip` `"<NEW_SESSION_LABEL or the whole title> · <statusWord(status)>"` plus `" · needs you"` with attention. `tabNeighbour`: the id after `closing` in `tabs`, else the one before, else null (null when `closing` is not there).
  - `SessionActions.close(id)` and `delete(id, title)`: when `id == manager.selected.value`, first `manager.select(tabNeighbour(tabs(), id))`, then today's call; the manager's own neighbour rule then finds nothing selected. The sidebar's Close and Delete already call these.
  - `SessionHandle.selfEnds` (deviation 27): `releaseLocked` returns whether it let the process go; the path where the process's own exit is seen (`process.running.join()`, then `release(session)`) increments the count when that release let it go and the handle is not closed; every other release (the stops, `handOver`, `failToStart`) never counts. `SessionManager.watch` gains a collector, beside the turn-end one: when `selfEnds` grows, `raiseAttentionUnlessSelected(handle)`. Logs: the session id only.
  - `SessionTabsController`: in `scope` (`close()` cancels it), it collects `combine(source.tabs, source.selected) { open, selected -> sessionTabs(open, selected) to selected }`, conflated, built on `compute`, and applies only the Swing calls on `apply`: for each tab missing a content, `ContentFactory.getInstance().createContent(panel, label, false)` with `setShouldDisposeContent(false)`, `isCloseable = true`, `description = tooltip`, `icon = SessionTabIcon(status, attention)`, `putUserData(ToolWindow.SHOW_CONTENT_ICON, true)` and the session id in its user data, added at the tab's index; a changed label, tooltip or icon set on its content; a content whose session left the tabs removed; then the selected session's content selected when it is not. While no tab exists, one placeholder content (the same panel, name `""`, `isCloseable = false`) is there, removed when the first tab arrives. Its own adds, removals and selections set a flag the listener checks, so they never call back. `ContentManagerListener`: `selectionChanged` with the add operation on a session's content that the controller did not select → `actions.show(id)` and `focus(id)`; `contentRemoveQuery` on a session's content that the controller is not removing → `event.consume()` and `actions.close(id)` (the tab leaves once the source drops the session). `tabs` holds the applied ids. Logs: adds, removals and selections by session id only, never titles.
  - `SessionTabIcon`: `JBUI.scale(12)` square; the dot and the attention ring of Global Constraints' exact values, colours read in `paintIcon` (so a theme change shows at the next paint).
  - Factory (spec 18.3): when not the rendering spike, `var compose: ComposePanel? = null`; `val panel = JewelComposePanel(focusOnClickInside = true, config = { isDisposeOnRemove = false; compose = this }) { TandemToolWindow(project, sessions) }`; `Disposer.register(toolWindow.disposable) { controller.close(); compose?.dispose() }`; the controller on `toolWindow.contentManager`, that panel, `managerTabsSource(sessions.manager)`, `TandemWindowService.get(project).actions` and `::requestFocus`, in `TandemProjectScope.get(project).scope`; `(toolWindow as? ToolWindowEx)?.setTabActions(ActionManager.getInstance().getAction(NEW_SESSION_ACTION_ID))`; `sessions.ensureStarted()` as today. The rendering spike's branch is unchanged. Tab reordering, DnD, split and move-to-window stay off (nothing sets them).
  - `TandemToolWindow`: `val actions = TandemWindowService.get(project).actions` in place of its own `SessionActions`, and a `LaunchedEffect(Unit)` collecting `focusRequests` into `sessionShown`. `SessionSidebar.StatusDot` takes its word from `statusWord`.
  - Fake: `TANDEM_FAKE_EXIT_AT_END=<ms>` (deviation 26): after the last replayed line, sleep that long, then exit 0; unset, as today; one line in the docstring's environment list.

- Test: `src/test/kotlin/dev/daarkolabs/tandem/session/SessionManagerTest.kt`, `.../protocol/FixtureDecodeTest.kt` (D0's fixture)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: D1 `SessionConfig.additionalDirectories`, `SystemInit.additionalDirectories`; D2a `ChatState.additionalDirectories`, `settled()`.
- Produces (spec 3.4, 4.2):
  ```kotlin
  data class AddedFolder(val path: String, val state: FolderState)                // SessionHandle.kt
  enum class FolderState { PENDING, ACTIVE, MISSING }
  class SessionHandle {
      val folders: StateFlow<List<AddedFolder>>
      internal fun addFolder(realPath: String): Boolean        // false when already listed (same string)
      internal fun removeFolder(realPath: String): Boolean     // (removal) also remembers it as removed; a later addFolder of it forgets that
      internal fun copyFolders(from: SessionHandle)            // all PENDING
      internal suspend fun foldersForSpawn(): List<String>     // on Dispatchers.IO: a folder that is no longer a directory leaves the list with the WARNING notice
                                                               // "The folder <path> no longer exists, so it was not added."; the rest in the order added, remembered as the spawn's
      internal fun spawnDiffers(): Boolean                     // a live process runs and its spawn's folders differ from folders' paths
      internal suspend fun confirmFolders(listed: List<String>) // on IO: per spawn folder, listed (string equality, then Files.isSameFile) → ACTIVE, else MISSING
                                                               // and the WARNING "The CLI did not add the folder <path>." once per folder per spawn;
                                                               // (removal) per removed folder not among the spawn's: listed (same comparison) → the WARNING
                                                               // "The CLI still lists the removed folder <path>." once per folder per spawn
  }
  class SessionManager {
      fun addFolder(id: SessionId, realPath: String)      // the caller checked it on IO; then respawnWhenIdle
      fun removeFolder(id: SessionId, realPath: String)   // then respawnWhenIdle
  }
  ```
- Rules: `respawnWhenIdle(handle)` keeps one job per handle in `handle.scope`: while `handle.spawnDiffers()`, it tries `handle.stopIfIdle()`, and on `false` waits for the next change of `handle.state` and tries again; it ends once the process is gone or the spawn no longer differs (so a folder added and removed before the stop stops nothing). No process → nothing to stop. `SessionSpawner.start` calls `handle.foldersForSpawn()` inside its existing IO block and passes the list as `configFor(…).additionalDirectories`. After each `system/init` (its `mcp.initCount` grows) of a process spawned with folders, or of any process once a folder was removed from the handle (the removal check must also run when the spawn has no folder left), the handle calls `confirmFolders(state.additionalDirectories)` on IO. (B) instead: the spawn's folders turn `ACTIVE` at its first init, never `MISSING`. `fork()` copies the open original's folders (fork **listed** in D0; else none), and so does `fork(id, atMessage)` (D12a; fork at a reply **listed** in D0; else none), passed after the fork tail (spec 13.1 "Added folders"); `create()` starts with none; folders live as long as the handle. Logs: counts only, never a path.
- Fake rule **(l)**: with `--add-dir <dir>` (repeatable) in its argv, every replayed `system/init` gets `additional_directories` = those dirs in order, less the one `TANDEM_FAKE_SKIP_ADD_DIR` names; `TANDEM_FAKE_KEEP_ADD_DIR=<dir>` (a CLI that keeps a removed folder) adds that dir to every replayed init, after the others and once, with or without `--add-dir`; with neither the line replays as recorded.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: D2b `SessionManager.addFolder/removeFolder`, `SessionHandle.folders`, `AddedFolder`, `FolderState`; D3 `displayPath`; D6a's menu.
- Produces (spec 3.4, 4.4):
  ```kotlin
  fun interface FolderChooser {
      fun choose(project: Project): Path?   // modal: called only from chooseLater, never from a Compose callback or a coroutine launched from composition
      fun chooseLater(project: Project, onChosen: (Path) -> Unit, launcher: (Runnable) -> Unit = { task ->
          ApplicationManager.getApplication().invokeLater(task, ModalityState.defaultModalityState(), project.disposed) }) =
          launcher(Runnable { choose(project)?.let(onChosen) })
      //   the base's ComposerImages.chooseImagesLater / chooseLater pattern (6cb1973): the chooser runs in an EDT event of its own after
      //   the menu's, in the default modality; a cancel passes nothing on; a disposed project opens nothing
  }
  object FolderChoosers { fun default(): FolderChooser; internal fun propertyFolder(): Path? }
  //   propertyFolder(): System.getProperty("tandem.addFolder")?.let(Path::of); default(): that path when set (IDE tests), else FileChooser.chooseFile(FileChooserDescriptorFactory.createSingleFolderDescriptor().withTitle("Add Folder"), project, null)?.toNioPath()
  sealed interface FolderCheck { data class Add(val realPath: String) : FolderCheck; data class Refused(val message: String) : FolderCheck }
  fun checkFolder(chosen: Path, projectRoot: Path, added: List<String>): FolderCheck   // blocking file checks: call on Dispatchers.IO
  class TandemSessions { val folderChooser: FolderChooser /* = FolderChoosers.default() */; internal val projectDirectory: Path }
  ```
- Check order (spec 3.4): not a directory → `Refused("Could not add $chosen: it is not a folder.")`; `real = chosen.toRealPath()`, `root = projectRoot.toRealPath()`; `real == root` or `real.startsWith(root)` (path segments, not strings) → `Refused("${real.fileName} is inside the project, so Claude can already use it.")`; `real.toString() in added` → `Refused("${real.fileName} is already added.")`; else `Add(real.toString())`.
- UI (spec 3.3–3.5, 3.7): page `PLUS_MAIN` gains `Add folder` (`Nodes.Folder` in the icon slot, as D6a's rows) after `Add files or photos`: the menu closes, then `sessions.folderChooser.chooseLater(project) { chosen -> scope.launch { val check = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { checkFolder(chosen, sessions.projectDirectory, handle.folders.value.map { f -> f.path }) }; Refused → ui.composerNotice.value = message; Add → manager.addFolder(handle.id, realPath) } }`. The modal chooser runs in an EDT event of its own, never inside the click or in `scope` (spec 3.4: the 0.1.1 picture freeze), and only the chosen path enters the coroutine, as the base's `attachFiles` does; the text area takes the focus once the chooser has closed. `FolderStrip` at the top of the composer box, above the image strip, collecting `handle.folders`: one pill per folder (`Nodes.Folder`, the folder's name, a 16 dp `×` `IconButton` with `contentDescription = "Remove folder <name>"` → `manager.removeFolder`, only when D0's removal is **dropped**, else no `×`), `contentDescription = "Folder <name>"`, the tooltip `displayPath(path, home, null)` and `Applies with the next prompt.` (PENDING), `Added to this session.` (ACTIVE) or `The CLI did not add this folder.` (MISSING, the pill in `text.error`). The `×` buttons sit in the box's focus order after `+`, before the text area.
